    26→
    27→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    28→// This is the new Agent interface method; delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    29→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    30→	return c.GetHookNames()
    31→}
    32→
    33→// ParseHookEvent translates a Claude Code hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    34→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    35→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNamePreTask:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePostTask:
    48→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    49→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    50→		// PostTodo is Claude-specific; handled outside the generic dispatcher.
    51→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    52→	default:
    53→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    54→	}
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    58→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    59→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→	return data, nil
    64→}
    65→
    66→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    67→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    68→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	var prompts []string
    74→	for i := range lines {
    75→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    79→		if content != "" {
    80→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→	return prompts, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    87→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    88→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    94→	if parseErr != nil {
    95→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
    99→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   100→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   101→			continue
   102→		}
   103→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   108→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   109→				return block.Text, nil
   110→			}
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return "", nil
   114→}
   115→
   116→// PrepareTranscript waits for Claude Code's async transcript flush to complete.
   117→// Claude writes a hook_progress sentinel entry after flushing all pending writes.
   118→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
   119→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   120→	return nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
   124→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   125→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/
   126→	// but we don't have the sessionID here. The caller should pass the transcript path
   127→	// which may contain the session ID in its directory structure.
   128→	// For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure.
   129→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   130→}

   132→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   133→
   134→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   135→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   136→	if err != nil {
   137→		return nil, err
   138→	}
   139→	return &agent.Event{
   140→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   141→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   142→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   143→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   144→	}, nil
   145→}
   146→
   147→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   148→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		return nil, err
   151→	}
   152→	return &agent.Event{
   153→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   154→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   155→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   156→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   157→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   158→	}, nil
   159→}
   160→

   162→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return nil, err
   165→	}
   166→	return &agent.Event{
   167→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   168→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   169→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,

   172→}
   173→
   174→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   175→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   176→	if err != nil {
   177→		return nil, err
   178→	}
   179→	return &agent.Event{

   186→
   187→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   188→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)
   189→	if err != nil {
   190→		return nil, err
   191→	}
   192→	return &agent.Event{
   193→		Type:       agent.SubagentStart,
   194→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   195→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   196→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   197→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   198→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   199→	}, nil
   200→}
   201→
   202→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   203→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return nil, err
   206→	}
   207→	event := &agent.Event{
   208→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   209→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   210→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   211→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   212→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   213→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   214→	}
   215→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   216→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   217→	}
   218→	return event, nil
   219→}
   220→
   221→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   222→
   223→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   224→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   225→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   226→
   227→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   228→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   229→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   230→	const (
   231→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   232→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   233→		tailBytes    = 4096
   234→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   235→	)
   236→
   237→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   238→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   239→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   240→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   241→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   242→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   243→			)
   244→			return
   245→		}
   246→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   247→	}
   248→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   249→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   250→	)
   251→}
   252→
   253→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   254→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   255→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		return false
   258→	}
   259→	defer f.Close()
   260→
   261→	info, err := f.Stat()
   262→	if err != nil {
   263→		return false
   264→	}
   265→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   266→	if offset < 0 {
   267→		offset = 0
   268→	}
   269→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   270→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   271→		return false
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   275→	for _, line := range lines {
   276→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   277→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→
   281→		var entry struct {
   282→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   283→		}
   284→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   285→			continue
   286→		}
   287→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   288→		if err != nil {
   289→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   290→			if err != nil {
   291→				continue
   292→			}
   293→		}
   294→		// Validate timestamp is within acceptable range:
   295→		// - Not too far in the past (before hook started minus skew)
   296→		// - Not too far in the future (after hook started plus skew)
   297→		lowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   298→		upperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)
   299→		if ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {
   300→			return true
   301→		}
   302→	}
   303→	return false
   304→}
   305→
```

   244→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   268→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse []ClaudeHookMatcher
   298→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   299→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   300→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   301→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   302→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   303→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   304→
   305→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   306→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   307→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   308→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   309→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   310→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   311→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   312→
   313→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   314→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   315→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   316→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   317→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   318→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   319→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   320→
   321→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   322→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   323→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   324→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   325→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   326→			rawPermissions = nil
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   331→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   332→			var denyRules []string
   333→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   334→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   335→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   336→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   337→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   338→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   339→					}
   340→				}
   341→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   342→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   343→					if err == nil {
   344→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   345→					}
   346→				} else {
   347→					// Remove empty deny array
   348→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   349→				}
   350→			}
   351→		}
   352→
   353→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   354→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   355→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   356→			if err == nil {
   357→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   358→			}
   359→		} else {
   360→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   361→		}
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   365→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   366→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   367→		if err != nil {
   368→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   369→		}
   370→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   371→	} else {
   372→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Write back
   376→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return nil
   384→}
   385→
   386→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   387→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   388→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   389→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   390→	if err != nil {
   391→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   392→	}
   393→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   394→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   395→	if err != nil {
   396→		return false
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   400→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   401→		return false
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   405→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   406→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   407→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   408→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   409→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   410→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   411→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   412→}
   413→
   414→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   415→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   416→	return []agent.HookType{
   417→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   418→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   419→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   420→		agent.HookStop,
   421→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   422→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   423→	}
   424→}
   425→
   426→// Helper functions for hook management
   427→
   428→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   431→			if hook.Command == command {
   432→				return true
   433→			}
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→	return false
   437→}
   438→
   439→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   440→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   441→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   442→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   443→				if hook.Command == command {
   444→					return true
   445→				}
   446→			}
   447→		}
   448→	}
   449→	return false
   450→}
   451→
   452→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   453→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   454→		Type:    "command",
   455→		Command: command,
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   459→	if matcherName == "" {
   460→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   461→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   462→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   463→				return matchers
   464→			}
   465→		}
   466→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   467→			Matcher: "",
   468→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   469→		})
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   473→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   474→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   475→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   476→			return matchers
   477→		}
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   481→		Matcher: matcherName,
   482→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   483→	})
   484→}
   485→
   486→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   487→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   488→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   489→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   490→			return true
   491→		}
   492→	}
   493→	return false
   494→}
   495→
   496→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   497→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   498→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   499→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   500→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   501→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   502→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   503→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   504→			}
   505→		}
   506→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   507→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   508→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   509→			result = append(result, matcher)
   510→		}
   511→	}
   512→	return result
   513→}
   514→
   515→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   516→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   517→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   518→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   519→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   520→}
   521→
```

    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its

    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:

    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript

   166→
   167→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.

   169→}

## Proposed Approaches

Given that Factory Droid's hook system is nearly identical to Claude Code's (same JSON config format, same JSONL transcripts, same stdin/stdout pattern, same env var approach), there's really one clear architectural path with a few design choices. Here are the approaches:

### Approach 1: Full Clone of Claude Code Pattern (Recommended)

Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryai/` mirroring the Claude Code structure exactly:
- Same file layout (`factoryai.go`, `lifecycle.go`, `hooks.go`, `types.go`, `transcript.go` + tests)
- Reuse the existing `transcript` package for JSONL parsing (since Factory Droid uses the same format)
- Implement all optional interfaces: `HookSupport`, `HookHandler`, `TranscriptAnalyzer`, `TranscriptPreparer`, `TokenCalculator`, `SubagentAwareExtractor`
- Hook config in `.factory/settings.json` (project-level)
- `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var for localDev mode

**Pro:** Proven pattern, full feature parity, minimal framework changes needed.
**Con:** Some code will be very similar to Claude Code (hook helpers especially). We can mitigate by extracting shared helpers if duplication gets high.

### Approach 2: Extract Common Hook Helpers First, Then Build

Before implementing Factory AI, extract the shared hook installation logic (JSON settings file parsing, idempotent hook addition/removal, `entireHookPrefixes`) into a reusable `hookutil` package. Then both Claude Code and Factory AI use it.

**Pro:** Less code duplication, cleaner architecture.
**Con:** Requires refactoring Claude Code first, increases scope and risk of regression.

### Approach 3: Minimal Adapter Over Claude Code

Since the formats are so similar, create a thin adapter that delegates transcript parsing to the existing Claude Code transcript logic, only overriding identity, hook names, and config paths.

**Pro:** Minimal new code.
**Con:** Creates a fragile coupling between agents. If Factory Droid's transcript format diverges, we'd need to untangle.

---

**My recommendation is Approach 1.** The patterns are well-established, the `implement-agent-guide.md` was literally written for this use case, and the duplication is manageable (mostly boilerplate hook helpers that are simple and stable). We can always extract shared helpers later once we see the real duplication patterns.

Does this approach sound right, or would you prefer one of the alternatives?

## Design: Factory AI Droid Agent Integration

### Section 1: Package Structure & Identity

```
cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryai/
├── factoryai.go          # Core Agent implementation + init() registration
├── lifecycle.go          # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions
├── types.go              # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
├── hooks.go              # HookSupport + HookHandler implementation
├── transcript.go         # TranscriptAnalyzer, TokenCalculator, SubagentAwareExtractor
├── lifecycle_test.go     # Tests for ParseHookEvent (all 9 hook types)
├── hooks_test.go         # Tests for hook install/uninstall/idempotency
└── transcript_test.go    # Tests for transcript analysis
```

**Identity:**
- `Name()` → `"factory-ai"` (registry key, used in CLI: `entire hooks factory-ai stop`)
- `Type()` → `"Factory AI"` (display name in metadata/trailers)
- `Description()` → `"Factory AI Droid - agent-native development platform"`
- `DetectPresence()` → checks for `.factory/` directory at repo root
- `ProtectedDirs()` → `[".factory"]`

### Section 2: Hook Event Mapping

This is the core of the integration — mapping Factory Droid's 9 native hooks to normalized lifecycle events:

| Factory Droid Hook | Lifecycle Event | Matcher | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SessionStart` | `SessionStart` | — | Also includes `source` field (startup/resume/clear/compact) |
| `UserPromptSubmit` | `TurnStart` | — | Includes `prompt` field |
| `Stop` | `TurnEnd` | — | Has `stop_hook_active` to prevent infinite loops |
| `SessionEnd` | `SessionEnd` | — | Includes `reason` field |
| `PreToolUse` | `SubagentStart` | `Task` | Only when matcher is "Task" (sub-droid spawn) |
| `PostToolUse` | `SubagentEnd` | `Task` | Only when matcher is "Task" |
| `SubagentStop` | *(pass-through)* | — | Return `nil, nil` — we use PostToolUse[Task] instead |
| `PreCompact` | `Compaction` | — | Maps to compaction event for transcript offset reset |
| `Notification` | *(pass-through)* | — | Return `nil, nil` — no lifecycle significance |

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Key difference from Claude Code:** Factory Droid has `SubagentStop` and `PreCompact` hooks that Claude Code doesn't. `PreCompact` maps to the `Compaction` event type (which exists in the framework but Claude Code doesn't use), while `SubagentStop` is a pass-through since we track subagent lifecycle via `PreToolUse[Task]`/`PostToolUse[Task]` instead.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

The hook names that become CLI subcommands:
```
entire hooks factoryai-droid session-start
entire hooks factoryai-droid session-end
entire hooks factoryai-droid stop
entire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit
entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use
entire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use
entire hooks factoryai-droid subagent-stop
entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact
entire hooks factoryai-droid notification
```

### Section 3: Hook Installation (HookSupport)

Factory Droid's settings file format is virtually identical to Claude Code's:

**Settings file location:** `.factory/settings.json` (project-level, at repo root)

**Hook config format:**
```json
{
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid session-start"}]}],
    "SessionEnd": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid session-end"}]}],
    "Stop": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid stop"}]}],
    "UserPromptSubmit": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit"}]}],
    "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use"}]}],
    "PostToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use"}]}],
    "PreCompact": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact"}]}]
  }
}
```

**localDev mode** uses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var:
```
go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid stop
```

**`entireHookPrefixes`** will include `"go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go "` for hook detection/removal.

**Metadata deny rule:** `Read(./.entire/metadata/**)` — same as Claude Code, prevents the Droid from reading Entire's internal metadata.

**Hooks we install (7 total):** SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task], PreCompact. We skip Notification and SubagentStop (pass-throughs).

### Section 4: Transcript Analysis

Since Factory Droid uses JSONL format (like Claude Code), we can reuse the existing `transcript` package for parsing. The key assumption is that the JSONL schema is compatible — if it differs, we'd create Factory-specific parsing.

**TranscriptAnalyzer:**
- `GetTranscriptPosition()` → line count (same as Claude Code)
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` → parse JSONL for file-modification tools
- `ExtractPrompts()` → extract user prompt entries
- `ExtractSummary()` → last assistant text block

**TranscriptPreparer:**
- Factory Droid likely has async transcript writing (like Claude Code)
- Implement `PrepareTranscript()` with a sentinel-polling approach
- The sentinel string would be `"hooks factoryai-droid stop"` (matching our hook command)

**TokenCalculator:**
- Extract token usage from JSONL entries (if Factory Droid includes usage data)
- Delegate to the same `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` pattern

**SubagentAwareExtractor:**
- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` — includes sub-droid transcripts
- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` — aggregates across sub-droids

**Important caveat:** The transcript analysis methods depend on the exact JSONL schema. We'll implement them assuming a Claude-Code-compatible format initially. If the schema differs, we'll need to adjust the parsing. We should add a TODO/note for this.

### Section 5: What We DON'T Need to Change

The framework is designed for agent pluggability, so no changes are needed to:
- `lifecycle.go` (dispatcher) — already handles all event types
- `hook_registry.go` — auto-discovers agents implementing `HookHandler`
- `strategy/` — strategies are agent-agnostic
- `setup.go` — auto-detects agents via `DetectPresence()`
- Integration tests — `RunForAllStrategies` will pick up the new agent

The only change outside the new package is a **blank import** in `cmd/entire/cli/commands/` to trigger the `init()` registration.

### Section 6: Testing Strategy

1. **`lifecycle_test.go`** — Test `ParseHookEvent` for all 9 hook names:
   - SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit → correct event types
   - PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task] → SubagentStart/End
   - SubagentStop, Notification → `nil, nil` (pass-through)
   - PreCompact → Compaction event
   - Unknown hook → `nil, nil`
   - Empty input → error
   - Malformed JSON → error

2. **`hooks_test.go`** — Test hook installation:
   - Creates `.factory/settings.json` with correct structure
   - Idempotent (second install returns count=0)
   - Force mode removes existing hooks first
   - Preserves unknown fields in settings file
   - Uninstall removes only Entire hooks
   - localDev mode uses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR`

3. **`transcript_test.go`** — Test transcript analysis:
   - Extract modified files from JSONL
   - Extract prompts
   - Extract summary
   - Token usage calculation
   - Chunking and reassembly

All tests use `t.Parallel()` per project conventions.

---

That's the complete design. Does this all look good to proceed with?

  "content": "# Factory AI Droid Agent Integration Design\n\n## Summary\n\nAdd a new agent integration for [Factory AI Droid](https://factory.ai/) to the Entire CLI. Factory Droid is an AI coding agent with a hook system nearly identical to Claude Code's: JSON config file, JSONL transcripts, stdin-based hook input, and the same lifecycle event types.\n\n## Scope\n\nFull feature parity with the Claude Code agent: all lifecycle hooks, HookSupport (auto-install), TranscriptAnalyzer, TranscriptPreparer, TokenCalculator, and SubagentAwareExtractor.\n\n## Identity\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Registry key (`Name()`) | `factoryai-droid` |\n| Display name (`Type()`) | `Factory AI Droid` |\n| Description | `Factory AI Droid - agent-native development platform` |\n| Detection | `.factory/` directory at repo root |\n| Protected dirs | `[\".factory\"]` |\n| Config file | `.factory/settings.json` (project-level) |\n| Env var (localDev) | `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` |\n\n## Package Structure\n\n```\ncmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/\n  factoryaidroid.go      # Core Agent implementation + init() registration\n  lifecycle.go           # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions\n  types.go               # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants\n  hooks.go               # HookSupport + HookHandler implementation\n  transcript.go          # TranscriptAnalyzer, TokenCalculator, SubagentAwareExtractor\n  lifecycle_test.go      # Tests for ParseHookEvent (all 9 hook types)\n  hooks_test.go          # Tests for hook install/uninstall/idempotency\n  transcript_test.go     # Tests for transcript analysis\n```\n\n## Hook Event Mapping\n\nFactory Droid supports 9 hook events. Mapping to normalized lifecycle events:\n\n| Factory Droid Hook | Lifecycle Event | Matcher | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `SessionStart` | `SessionStart` | -- | `source` field: startup/resume/clear/compact |\n| `UserPromptSubmit` | `TurnStart` | -- | Includes `prompt` field |\n| `Stop` | `TurnEnd` | -- | Has `stop_hook_active` field |\n| `SessionEnd` | `SessionEnd` | -- | Includes `reason` field |\n| `PreToolUse` | `SubagentStart` | `Task` | Sub-droid spawn |\n| `PostToolUse` | `SubagentEnd` | `Task` | Sub-droid completion |\n| `SubagentStop` | *(pass-through)* | -- | `nil, nil` return |\n| `PreCompact` | `Compaction` | -- | Context window compression |\n| `Notification` | *(pass-through)* | -- | `nil, nil` return |\n\n### CLI Subcommands\n\n```\nentire hooks factoryai-droid session-start\nentire hooks factoryai-droid session-end\nentire hooks factoryai-droid stop\nentire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit\nentire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use\nentire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use\nentire hooks factoryai-droid subagent-stop\nentire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact\nentire hooks factoryai-droid notification\n```\n\n## Hook Installation\n\n### Installed Hooks (7 total)\n\nSessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task], PreCompact. Notification and SubagentStop are not installed (pass-throughs).\n\n### Settings File Format\n\n```json\n{\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"SessionStart\": [{\"matcher\": \"\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid session-start\"}]}],\n    \"SessionEnd\": [{\"matcher\": \"\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid session-end\"}]}],\n    \"Stop\": [{\"matcher\": \"\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid stop\"}]}],\n    \"UserPromptSubmit\": [{\"matcher\": \"\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit\"}]}],\n    \"PreToolUse\": [{\"matcher\": \"Task\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use\"}]}],\n    \"PostToolUse\": [{\"matcher\": \"Task\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use\"}]}],\n    \"PreCompact\": [{\"matcher\": \"\", \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact\"}]}]\n  },\n  \"permissions\": {\n    \"deny\": [\"Read(./.entire/metadata/**)\"]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### localDev Mode\n\nUses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var:\n```\ngo run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid stop\n```\n\n## Transcript Analysis\n\nFactory Droid uses JSONL format (like Claude Code). Reuses the existing `transcript` package for parsing.\n\n**Assumption:** The JSONL schema is compatible with Claude Code's format. If it differs, Factory-specific parsing will be needed.\n\n### TranscriptAnalyzer\n\n- `GetTranscriptPosition()` -- line count\n- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` -- parse JSONL for Excellent! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile a comprehensive summary of the implementation details for the plan:

## Summary: Implementation Details for Adding a New Agent to Entire CLI

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the complete implementation details you'll need for writing the plan:

---

### 1. **Blank Import Registration Locations**

Agent blank imports are registered in **two key files**:

- **`/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/config.go`** (line 15)
  - Imports `claudecode` agent
  - Comment: "Import claudecode to register the agent"
  - Used by non-hook code to ensure the default agent is registered

- **`/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go`** (lines 6-7)
  - Imports both `claudecode` and `geminicli` agents
  - Comment: "Import agents to ensure they are registered before we iterate"
  - Used by `newHooksCmd()` to dynamically create agent hook subcommands
  - Iterates `agent.List()` to find all registered agents that implement `HookHandler`

**Pattern**: Use `_ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/<agentname>"` in init() functions within the agent package to auto-register via the registry.

---

### 2. **Agent Registry & Constants** (`registry.go`)

**Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go`

**Key Components**:

- **AgentName type** (string): Registry key (lowercase, hyphenated)
  - `AgentNameClaudeCode = "claude-code"`
  - `AgentNameGemini = "gemini"`

- **AgentType type** (string): Display name for metadata/trailers (title-cased)
  - `AgentTypeClaudeCode = "Claude Code"`
  - `AgentTypeGemini = "Gemini CLI"`
  - `AgentTypeUnknown = "Agent"` (fallback for backward compatibility)

- **DefaultAgentName**: `AgentNameClaudeCode`

- **Factory function type**: `type Factory func() Agent`

- **Registry functions**:
  - `Register(name AgentName, factory Factory)` - Called from init() in each agent
  - `Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error)` - Retrieve agent by name
  - `List() []AgentName` - Get all registered agents (sorted)
  - `Detect() (Agent, error)` - Auto-detect via DetectPresence
  - `DetectAll() []Agent` - Return all agents with DetectPresence = true
  - `GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error)` - Lookup by display type
  - `AllProtectedDirs() []string` - Union of all agent protected dirs
  - `Default() Agent` - Return default agent (DefaultAgentName)

---

### 3. **Agent Interface Definition** (`agent.go`)

**Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`

**Core Agent Interface** (11 required methods + optional interfaces):

**Identity Methods (5)**:
```go
Name() AgentName                          // Registry key
Type() AgentType                          // Display type
Description() string                      // Human-readable description
DetectPresence() (bool, error)            // Check if configured in repo
ProtectedDirs() []string                  // Repo-root-relative dirs to protect (e.g., [".gemini"])
```

**Event Mapping Methods (2)**:
```go
HookNames() []string                      // Hook verbs this agent supports
ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)  // Translate hook → normalized Event
```

**Transcript Storage Methods (3)**:
```go
ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
```

**Legacy Methods (8)** - Will be moved to optional interfaces:
```go
GetHookConfigPath() string
SupportsHooks() bool
ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
```

**Optional Interfaces** (implement as needed):

- **HookSupport**: Install/uninstall hooks, check if installed
- **HookHandler**: Get hook names (deprecated, use HookNames() instead)
- **TranscriptAnalyzer**: Parse transcripts to extract files, prompts, summaries
- **TranscriptPreparer**: Ensure transcript ready before reading
- **TokenCalculator**: Compute token usage from transcript
- **FileWatcher**: File-based session detection (for non-hook agents)
- **SubagentAwareExtractor**: Extract subagent files and tokens

---

### 4. **Gemini CLI Agent Structure** 

**Directory**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/`

**Files**:
- **types.go** - Data structures for hook configs and transcript parsing
- **gemini.go** - Core agent implementation (Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, etc.)
- **lifecycle.go** - Event parsing (ParseHookEvent, internal hook handlers)
- **hooks.go** - Hook installation/management
- **transcript.go** - Transcript parsing and analysis

#### types.go - Gemini-Specific Structures

```go
// Settings structure (mirrors agent's config file format)
type GeminiSettings struct {
    HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig  // {enabled: bool}
    Hooks       GeminiHooks        // Hook matchers by type
}

// Hook matchers
type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    Matcher string                 // Matcher pattern (empty for default, "*" for all)
    Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry      // Actual hook entries
}

type GeminiHookEntry struct {
    Name    string                 // Required: "entire-<hook-name>" (unlike Claude)
    Type    string                 // "command"
    Command string                 // "entire hooks gemini <hook-name>" or local dev path
}

// Hook input structures (JSON from .gemini hooks)
type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    SessionID      string           // Session UUID
    TranscriptPath string           // Path to session JSON file
    Cwd            string           // Current working directory
    HookEventName  string           // e.g., "SessionStart"
    Timestamp      string           // ISO 8601 timestamp
    Source         string           // "startup", "resume", "clear" (SessionStart only)
    Reason         string           // "exit", "logout" (SessionEnd only)
}

type agentHookInputRaw struct {
    SessionID      string
    TranscriptPath string
    Cwd            string
    HookEventName  string
    Timestamp      string
    Prompt         string           // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
}

type toolHookInputRaw struct {
    SessionID      string
    TranscriptPath string
    Cwd            string
    HookEventName  string
    Timestamp      string
    ToolName       string
    ToolInput      json.RawMessage
    ToolResponse   json.RawMessage  // AfterTool only
}

// Gemini transcript structure (JSON format, not JSONL)
type GeminiTranscript struct {
    Messages []GeminiMessage
}

type GeminiMessage struct {
    ID        string
    Type      string              // "user" or "gemini"
    Content   string              // Unmarshaled from both string and array formats
    ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall
}

type GeminiToolCall struct {
    ID     string
    Name   string                 // "write_file", "edit_file", "replace", "save_file"
    Args   map[string]interface{} // File path in "file_path", "path", or "filename" key
    Status string
}

// Tool names (Gemini uses different names than Claude)
const (
    ToolWriteFile = "write_file"
    ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    ToolReplace   = "replace"
)

// Token usage (Gemini format)
type geminiMessageTokens struct {
    Input    int
    Output   int
    Cached   int
    Thoughts int
    Tool     int
    Total    int
}
```

#### Hook Names (10 hooks in Gemini CLI)

```go
const (
    HookNameSessionStart        = "session-start"
    HookNameSessionEnd          = "session-end"
    HookNameBeforeAgent         = "before-agent"      // Maps to UserPromptSubmit
    HookNameAfterAgent          = "after-agent"       // Maps to TurnEnd
    HookNameBeforeModel         = "before-model"      // Pass-through
    HookNameAfterModel          = "after-model"       // Pass-through
    HookNameBeforeToolSelection = "before-tool-selection"  // Pass-through
    HookNameBeforeTool          = "before-tool"       // Maps to PreToolUse
    HookNameAfterTool           = "after-tool"        // Maps to PostToolUse
    HookNamePreCompress         = "pre-compress"      // Maps to Compaction
    HookNameNotification        = "notification"      // Pass-through
)
```

#### gemini.go - Core Implementation

Key methods:
- `DetectPresence()` - Checks for `.gemini` directory or `.gemini/settings.json`
- `GetHookConfigPath()` - Returns `.gemini/settings.json`
- `GetSessionDir(repoPath)` - Returns `~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-path>/chats/`
- `ResolveSessionFile()` - Looks for `session-*-<first8chars>.json` (Gemini's naming convention)
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` - Parses JSON transcript from message index N, extracts files from tool calls
- `ChunkTranscript()` - Splits JSON message array across chunks
- `ReassembleTranscript()` - Merges message arrays from chunks

#### lifecycle.go - Event Parsing

Translates Gemini hooks to normalized events:
```go
ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
// Maps:
// - SessionStart → Event{Type: SessionStart, ...}
// - BeforeAgent → Event{Type: TurnStart, Prompt: ..., ...}
// - AfterAgent → Event{Type: TurnEnd, ...}
// - SessionEnd → Event{Type: SessionEnd, ...}
// - PreCompress → Event{Type: Compaction, ...}
// - Others → nil (pass-through, no lifecycle action)
```

Helper methods for parsing specific hook types:
- `parseSessionStart(stdin)`
- `parseTurnStart(stdin)` - BeforeAgent
- `parseTurnEnd(stdin)` - AfterAgent
- `parseSessionEnd(stdin)`
- `parseCompaction(stdin)` - PreCompress

Also implements:
- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset)` - Extract all user prompts
- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef)` - Last assistant message
- `CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset)` - Sum token counts from messages

#### hooks.go - Hook Installation

```go
InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
// Installs 12 hooks total:
// - session-start (1)
// - session-end with exit/logout matchers (2)
// - before-agent, after-agent (2)
// - before-model, after-model (2)
// - before-tool-selection (1)
// - before-tool, after-tool with "*" matcher (2)
// - pre-compress (1)
// - notification (1)

UninstallHooks() error
AreHooksInstalled() bool
GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
```

Helper functions:
- `parseGeminiHookType()` / `marshalGeminiHookType()` - Preserve unknown hook types
- `addGeminiHook()` - Add entry to matcher (unlike Claude, requires "name" field)
- `isEntireHook()` / `hasEntireHook()` / `getFirstEntireHookCommand()` - Check for Entire prefixes
- `removeEntireHooks()` - Clean up existing hooks before reinstalling

**Gemini Settings File Format** (`.gemini/settings.json`):
```json
{
  "hooksConfig": {
    "enabled": true
  },
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": "",
        "hooks": [
          {
            "name": "entire-session-start",
            "type": "command",
            "command": "entire hooks gemini session-start"
          }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "SessionEnd": [...],
    ...
  }
}
```

#### transcript.go - Transcript Parsing

Key functions:
- `ParseTranscript(data)` - Unmarshal JSON to GeminiTranscript
- `ExtractModifiedFiles(data)` / `ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript(t)` - Extract files
- `ExtractLastUserPrompt()` / `ExtractAllUserPrompts()` - Get user messages
- `ExtractLastAssistantMessage()` - Get last Gemini response
- `GetLastMessageID()` - Get ID of last message
- `SliceFromMessage(data, startIndex)` - Scope transcript from message index
- `CalculateTokenUsage(data, startIndex)` - Sum tokens from messages

---

### 5. **Hook Registry & Command Auto-Registration** (`hook_registry.go`)

**Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go`

**How It Works**:

The `newHooksCmd()` function dynamically creates agent-specific hook subcommands:

```go
func newHooksCmd() *cobra.Command {
    cmd := &cobra.Command{
        Use:    "hooks",
        Short:  "Hook handlers",
        Long:   "Commands called by hooks. These are internal and not for direct user use.",
        Hidden: true,
    }

    cmd.AddCommand(newHooksGitCmd())

    // Dynamically add agent hook subcommands
    for _, agentName := range agent.List() {  // Sorted iteration
        ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
        if handler, ok := ag.(agent.HookHandler); ok {
            cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHooksCmd(agentName, handler))
        }
    }

    return cmd
}
```

For each agent that implements `HookHandler`:

1. `newAgentHooksCmd()` creates a subcommand named after the agent (e.g., `entire hooks gemini`)
2. For each hook in `agent.GetHookNames()`, creates a leaf command (e.g., `entire hooks gemini session-start`)
3. Each leaf command runs `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` which:
   - Parses the hook event via `agent.ParseHookEvent()`
   - Dispatches to `DispatchLifecycleEvent()` if event is non-nil
   - Handles Claude-specific `PostTodo` directly
   - Logs duration and success/failure

**Flow**:
```
entire hooks <agent-name> <hook-name>
    ↓
newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()
    ↓
agent.ParseHookEvent(hookName, stdin)  ← Agent translates native hook format
    ↓
DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)      ← Framework handles lifecycle
```

---

### 6. **Transcript Package** (`transcript/`)

**Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript/`

**File**: `types.go` - Shared types for Claude Code JSONL format:

```go
// Message type constants
const (
    TypeUser      = "user"
    TypeAssistant = "assistant"
)

// Content block types
const (
    ContentTypeText    = "text"
    ContentTypeToolUse = "tool_use"
)

// Line structure (single JSONL line)
type Line struct {
    Type    string          // "user" or "assistant"
    UUID    string
    Message json.RawMessage
}

// Message structures
type UserMessage struct {
    Content interface{}  // Can be string or []interface{} (array of text blocks)
}

type AssistantMessage struct {
    Content []ContentBlock
}

type ContentBlock struct {
    Type  string
    Text  string
    Name  string                 // Tool name (for tool_use blocks)
    Input json.RawMessage        // Tool input JSON
}

// Tool input structure (shared across agents)
type ToolInput struct {
    FilePath      string
    NotebookPath  string
    Description   string
    Command       string
    Pattern       string
    Skill         string
    URL           string
    Prompt        string
}
```

**File**: `parse.go` - Parsing functions:

```go
ParseFromBytes(content []byte) ([]Line, error)
ParseFromFileAtLine(path string, startLine int) ([]Line, int, error)
SliceFromLine(content []byte, startLine int) []byte
ExtractUserContent(message json.RawMessage) string
```

**Key Pattern**: Agent-agnostic shared utilities for JSONL transcript handling (Claude Code uses JSONL, Gemini uses JSON and provides its own parsers).

---

### 7. **Event Types** (`event.go`)

**Location**: `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go`

**Event Type Enum**:
```go
const (
    SessionStart   EventType = iota + 1
    TurnStart                                    // User submitted prompt
    TurnEnd                                      // Agent finished responding
    Compaction                                   // Agent about to compress context
    SessionEnd
    SubagentStart                                // Subagent spawned
    SubagentEnd                                  // Subagent completed
)
```

**Event Structure**:
```go
type Event struct {
    Type            EventType
    SessionID       string
    PreviousSessionID string                  // For session continuation
    SessionRef      string                    // Transcript path
    Prompt          string                    // User prompt (TurnStart)
    Timestamp       time.Time
    ToolUseID       string                    // Subagent invocation ID
    SubagentID      string                    // Subagent instance ID
    ToolInput       json.RawMessage           // Raw tool input
    ResponseMessage string                    // Optional message to agent
    Metadata        map[string]string         // Agent-specific state
}
```

**Helper**:
```go
ReadAndParseHookInput[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error)
// Generic unmarshaler for agent hook input JSON
```

---

### 8. **Claude Code Pattern** (for reference)

**Files**: Same structure as Gemini
- `types.go` - Claude-specific hook/message structures
- `claude.go` - Core agent implementation
- `lifecycle.go` - Event parsing
- `hooks.go` - Hook installation
- `transcript.go` - JSONL parsing

**Key Differences from Gemini**:
- Uses `.claude/` instead of `.gemini/`
- Uses `.claude/settings.json` with different structure (no hooksConfig section)
- Transcripts are JSONL format (Claude Code uses line-by-line)
- Hook entries don't require "name" field
- 6 core hooks (not 11) - no model/compression/notification hooks
- Session storage in `~/.claude/tmp/<hash>/sessions/`

---

### 9. **Integration Points Summary**

**To add a new agent**:

1. **Create package** at `cmd/entire/cli/agent/<agentname>/`

2. **Implement core files**:
   - `types.go` - Settings and hook structures
   - `<agent>.go` - Main implementation, init() with Register()
   - `lifecycle.go` - ParseHookEvent() translations
   - `hooks.go` - InstallHooks(), UninstallHooks(), AreHooksInstalled()
   - `transcript.go` - Transcript parsing, ExtractModifiedFiles(), CalculateTokenUsage()

3. **Register in**:
   - `cmd/entire/cli/config.go` - Add blank import (if not hook-only)
   - `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go` - Add blank import (if supports hooks)

4. **Run command auto-discovery**:
   - `newHooksCmd()` iterates `agent.List()` and auto-creates subcommands
   - Leaf commands call `agent.ParseHookEvent()` with stdin
   - Framework calls `DispatchLifecycleEvent()` with normalized Event

5. **Tests**: Add to `setup_test.go`, `rewind_test.go`, `mid_turn_commit_test.go` with blank imports

---

   101→// GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled returns names of agents that have hooks installed.
   102→func GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled() []agent.AgentName {
   103→	var installed []agent.AgentName
   104→	for _, name := range agent.List() {
   105→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   106→		if err != nil {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		if hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); ok && hs.AreHooksInstalled() {
   110→			installed = append(installed, name)
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return installed
   114→}
   115→
   116→// JoinAgentNames joins agent names into a comma-separated string.
   117→func JoinAgentNames(names []agent.AgentName) string {
   118→	strs := make([]string, len(names))
   119→	for i, n := range names {
   120→		strs[i] = string(n)
   121→	}
   122→	return strings.Join(strs, ",")
   123→}

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→)
    97→
    98→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
    99→const (
   100→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   101→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   102→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   103→)
   104→
   105→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   106→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   107→
   108→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   109→//
   110→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   111→// This is acceptable because:
   112→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   113→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   114→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   115→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   116→//
   117→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   118→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   119→	registryMu.RLock()
   120→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   121→
   122→	for _, factory := range registry {
   123→		ag := factory()
   124→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   125→			return ag, nil
   126→		}
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   130→}
   131→
   132→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   133→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   134→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   135→	registryMu.RLock()
   136→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   137→	for _, f := range registry {
   138→		factories = append(factories, f)
   139→	}
   140→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   141→
   142→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   143→	var dirs []string
   144→	for _, factory := range factories {
   145→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   146→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   147→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   148→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   149→			}
   150→		}
   151→	}
   152→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   153→	return dirs
   154→}
   155→
   156→// Default returns the default agent.
   157→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   158→//
   159→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   160→func Default() Agent {
   161→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   162→	return a
   163→}
   164→

     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"

    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    15→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    16→
    17→// Type aliases for internal use.
    18→type (
    19→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    20→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
    21→	toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
    22→)
    23→
    24→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes
    25→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    26→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    27→	for _, line := range lines {
    28→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    29→		if err != nil {
    30→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    31→		}
    32→		buf.Write(data)
    33→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    34→	}
    35→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript
    39→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    40→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    41→	var files []string
    42→
    43→	for _, line := range lines {
    44→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    45→			continue
    46→		}
    47→
    48→		var msg assistantMessage
    49→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    50→			continue
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    54→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    55→				continue
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    59→			isModifyTool := false
    60→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    61→				if block.Name == name {
    62→					isModifyTool = true
    63→					break
    64→				}
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			if !isModifyTool {
    68→				continue
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			var input toolInput
    72→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    73→				continue
    74→			}
    75→
    76→			file := input.FilePath
    77→			if file == "" {
    78→				file = input.NotebookPath
    79→			}
    80→
    81→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    82→				fileSet[file] = true
    83→				files = append(files, file)
    84→			}
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	return files
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript
    92→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines []TranscriptLine) string {
    93→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
    94→		if lines[i].Type != "user" { //nolint:goconst // already present in codebase
    95→			continue
    96→		}
    97→
    98→		var msg userMessage
    99→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   100→			continue
   101→		}
   102→
   103→		// Handle string content
   104→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   105→			return str
   106→		}
   107→
   108→		// Handle array content (text blocks)
   109→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   110→			var texts []string
   111→			for _, item := range arr {
   112→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   113→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   114→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   115→							texts = append(texts, text)
   116→						}
   117→					}
   118→				}
   119→			}
   120→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   121→				return strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")
   122→			}
   123→		}
   124→	}
   125→	return ""
   126→}
   127→
   128→// TruncateAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the line with given UUID
   129→func TruncateAtUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, uuid string) []TranscriptLine {
   130→	if uuid == "" {
   131→		return lines
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	for i, line := range lines {
   135→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   136→			return lines[:i+1]
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   141→	return lines
   142→}
   143→
   144→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   145→type toolResultBlock struct {
   146→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   147→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   151→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   152→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   153→}
   154→
   155→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   156→// for the given tool_use_id
   157→func FindCheckpointUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   158→	for _, line := range lines {
   159→		if line.Type != "user" {
   160→			continue
   161→		}
   162→
   163→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   164→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   165→			continue
   166→		}
   167→
   168→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   169→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   170→				return line.UUID, true
   171→			}
   172→		}
   173→	}
   174→	return "", false
   175→}
   176→
   177→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript.
   178→// This is specific to Claude/Anthropic's API format where each assistant message
   179→// contains a usage object with input_tokens, output_tokens, and cache tokens.
   180→//
   181→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   182→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   183→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcript []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   184→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   185→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   186→
   187→	for _, line := range transcript {
   188→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   189→			continue
   190→		}
   191→
   192→		var msg messageWithUsage
   193→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   194→			continue
   195→		}
   196→
   197→		if msg.ID == "" {
   198→			continue
   199→		}
   200→
   201→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   202→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   203→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   204→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Sum up all unique messages
   209→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   210→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   211→	}
   212→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   213→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   214→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   215→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   216→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return usage
   220→}
   221→
   222→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript file.
   223→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   224→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   225→	if path == "" {
   226→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)
   230→	if err != nil {
   231→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // caller adds context
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	return CalculateTokenUsage(lines), nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   238→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   239→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   240→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   241→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   242→
   243→	for _, line := range transcript {
   244→		if line.Type != "user" {
   245→			continue
   246→		}
   247→
   248→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   249→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   250→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   251→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   252→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   253→		}
   254→
   255→		var msg struct {
   256→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   257→		}
   258→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   259→			continue
   260→		}
   261→
   262→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   263→			continue
   264→		}
   265→
   266→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   267→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   268→				continue
   269→			}
   270→
   271→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   272→			var textContent string
   273→
   274→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   275→			var textBlocks []struct {
   276→				Type string `json:"type"`
   277→				Text string `json:"text"`
   278→			}
   279→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   280→				var textContentSb361 strings.Builder
   281→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   282→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   283→						textContentSb361.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   284→					}
   285→				}
   286→				textContent += textContentSb361.String()
   287→			} else {
   288→				// Try as plain string
   289→				var str string
   290→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   291→					textContent = str
   292→				}
   293→			}
   294→
   295→			// Look for agentId in the text
   296→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   297→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   298→			}
   299→		}
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	return agentIDs
   303→}
   304→
   305→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   306→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   307→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   308→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   309→	if idx == -1 {
   310→		return ""
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   314→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   315→	end := start
   316→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   317→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   318→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   319→		end++
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	if end > start {
   323→		return text[start:end]
   324→	}
   325→	return ""
   326→}
   327→
   328→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   329→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   330→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   331→func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   332→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   333→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Parse transcript ONCE
   337→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   338→	if err != nil {
   339→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   343→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(parsed)
   344→
   345→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   346→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   347→
   348→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   349→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   350→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   351→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   352→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   353→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   354→			if err != nil {
   355→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   356→				continue
   357→			}
   358→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   359→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   360→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   361→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   362→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   363→		}
   364→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   365→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   366→		}
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	return mainUsage, nil
   370→}
   371→
   372→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   373→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   374→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   375→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   376→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   377→func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   378→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   379→		return nil, nil
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	// Parse main transcript once
   383→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   384→	if err != nil {
   385→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// Collect modified files from main agent
   389→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   390→	var files []string
   391→	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(parsed) {
   392→		if !fileSet[f] {
   393→			fileSet[f] = true
   394→			files = append(files, f)
   395→		}
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   399→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   400→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   401→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   402→		agentLines, _, agentErr := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(agentPath, 0)
   403→		if agentErr != nil {
   404→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   405→			continue
   406→		}
   407→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentLines) {
   408→			if !fileSet[f] {
   409→				fileSet[f] = true
   410→				files = append(files, f)
   411→			}
   412→		}
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	return files, nil
   416→}
   417→

     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"time"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.
    13→var (
    14→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)
    15→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)
    16→)
    17→
    18→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Gemini CLI supports.
    19→// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    20→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) HookNames() []string {
    21→	return g.GetHookNames()
    22→}
    23→
    24→// ParseHookEvent translates a Gemini CLI hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    25→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    26→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    27→	switch hookName {
    28→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    29→		return g.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    30→	case HookNameBeforeAgent:
    31→		return g.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameAfterAgent:
    33→		return g.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    35→		return g.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNamePreCompress:
    37→		return g.parseCompaction(stdin)
    38→	case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
    39→		HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
    40→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    41→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    42→	default:
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    44→	}
    45→}
    46→
    47→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSON transcript bytes for a session.
    48→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    49→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return data, nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    57→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    58→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	t, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
    64→	if parseErr != nil {
    65→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	var prompts []string
    69→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(t.Messages); i++ {
    70→		msg := t.Messages[i]
    71→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
    72→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
    73→		}
    74→	}
    75→	return prompts, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    80→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data)
    85→}
    86→
    87→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    88→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
    89→	return CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    90→}
    91→

    94→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    95→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    96→	if err != nil {
    97→		return nil, err
    98→	}
    99→	return &agent.Event{
   100→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   101→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   102→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   103→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   104→	}, nil
   105→}
   106→
