   108→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentHookInputRaw](stdin)
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return nil, err
   111→	}
   112→	return &agent.Event{
   113→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   114→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   115→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   116→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   117→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   118→	}, nil
   119→}
   120→

     1→# Factory AI Droid Agent Integration Design
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→
     5→Add a new agent integration for [Factory AI Droid](https://factory.ai/) to the Entire CLI. Factory Droid is an AI coding agent with a hook system nearly identical to Claude Code's: JSON config file, JSONL transcripts, stdin-based hook input, and the same lifecycle event types.
     6→
     7→## Scope
     8→
     9→Full feature parity with the Claude Code agent: all lifecycle hooks, HookSupport (auto-install), TranscriptAnalyzer, TranscriptPreparer, TokenCalculator, and SubagentAwareExtractor.
    10→
    11→## Identity
    12→
    13→| Field | Value |
    14→|-------|-------|
    15→| Registry key (`Name()`) | `factoryai-droid` |
    16→| Display name (`Type()`) | `Factory AI Droid` |
    17→| Description | `Factory AI Droid - agent-native development platform` |
    18→| Detection | `.factory/` directory at repo root |
    19→| Protected dirs | `[".factory"]` |
    20→| Config file | `.factory/settings.json` (project-level) |
    21→| Env var (localDev) | `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` |
    22→
    23→## Package Structure
    24→
    25→```
    26→cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/
    27→  factoryaidroid.go      # Core Agent implementation + init() registration
    28→  lifecycle.go           # ParseHookEvent + compile-time assertions
    29→  types.go               # Hook input structs, transcript types, tool constants
    30→  hooks.go               # HookSupport + HookHandler implementation
    31→  transcript.go          # TranscriptAnalyzer, TokenCalculator, SubagentAwareExtractor
    32→  lifecycle_test.go      # Tests for ParseHookEvent (all 9 hook types)
    33→  hooks_test.go          # Tests for hook install/uninstall/idempotency
    34→  transcript_test.go     # Tests for transcript analysis
    35→```
    36→
    37→## Hook Event Mapping
    38→
    39→Factory Droid supports 9 hook events. Mapping to normalized lifecycle events:
    40→
    41→| Factory Droid Hook | Lifecycle Event | Matcher | Notes |
    42→|---|---|---|---|
    43→| `SessionStart` | `SessionStart` | -- | `source` field: startup/resume/clear/compact |
    44→| `UserPromptSubmit` | `TurnStart` | -- | Includes `prompt` field |
    45→| `Stop` | `TurnEnd` | -- | Has `stop_hook_active` field |
    46→| `SessionEnd` | `SessionEnd` | -- | Includes `reason` field |
    47→| `PreToolUse` | `SubagentStart` | `Task` | Sub-droid spawn |
    48→| `PostToolUse` | `SubagentEnd` | `Task` | Sub-droid completion |
    49→| `SubagentStop` | *(pass-through)* | -- | `nil, nil` return |
    50→| `PreCompact` | `Compaction` | -- | Context window compression |
    51→| `Notification` | *(pass-through)* | -- | `nil, nil` return |
    52→
    53→### CLI Subcommands
    54→
    55→```
    56→entire hooks factoryai-droid session-start
    57→entire hooks factoryai-droid session-end
    58→entire hooks factoryai-droid stop
    59→entire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit
    60→entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use
    61→entire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use
    62→entire hooks factoryai-droid subagent-stop
    63→entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact
    64→entire hooks factoryai-droid notification
    65→```
    66→
    67→## Hook Installation
    68→
    69→### Installed Hooks (7 total)
    70→
    71→SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task], PreCompact. Notification and SubagentStop are not installed (pass-throughs).
    72→
    73→### Settings File Format
    74→
    75→```json
    76→{
    77→  "hooks": {
    78→    "SessionStart": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid session-start"}]}],
    79→    "SessionEnd": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid session-end"}]}],
    80→    "Stop": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid stop"}]}],
    81→    "UserPromptSubmit": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid user-prompt-submit"}]}],
    82→    "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-tool-use"}]}],
    83→    "PostToolUse": [{"matcher": "Task", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid post-tool-use"}]}],
    84→    "PreCompact": [{"matcher": "", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks factoryai-droid pre-compact"}]}]
    85→  },
    86→  "permissions": {
    87→    "deny": ["Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"]
    88→  }
    89→}
    90→```
    91→
    92→### localDev Mode
    93→
    94→Uses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var:
    95→```
    96→go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid stop
    97→```
    98→
    99→## Transcript Analysis
   100→
   101→Factory Droid uses JSONL format (like Claude Code). Reuses the existing `transcript` package for parsing.
   102→
   103→**Assumption:** The JSONL schema is compatible with Claude Code's format. If it differs, Factory-specific parsing will be needed.
   104→
   105→### TranscriptAnalyzer
   106→
   107→- `GetTranscriptPosition()` -- line count
   108→- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` -- parse JSONL for file-modification tools
   109→- `ExtractPrompts()` -- extract user prompt entries
   110→- `ExtractSummary()` -- last assistant text block
   111→
   112→### TranscriptPreparer
   113→
   114→- `PrepareTranscript()` -- sentinel-polling for async flush
   115→- Sentinel string: `"hooks factoryai-droid stop"`
   116→
   117→### TokenCalculator
   118→
   119→- Extract token usage from JSONL entries (if Factory Droid includes usage data)
   120→
   121→### SubagentAwareExtractor
   122→
   123→- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` -- includes sub-droid transcripts
   124→- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` -- aggregates across sub-droids
   125→
   126→## Changes Outside New Package
   127→
   128→Only one change: blank import in `cmd/entire/cli/commands/` to trigger `init()` registration:
   129→
   130→```go
   131→import _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid"
   132→```
   133→
   134→No changes needed to lifecycle dispatcher, hook registry, strategies, setup, or integration tests.
   135→
   136→## Testing Strategy
   137→
   138→### lifecycle_test.go
   139→
   140→- All 9 hook names mapped correctly
   141→- Pass-through hooks (SubagentStop, Notification) return `nil, nil`
   142→- PreCompact returns Compaction event
   143→- Unknown hooks return `nil, nil`
   144→- Empty input and malformed JSON return errors
   145→
   146→### hooks_test.go
   147→
   148→- Creates `.factory/settings.json` with correct structure
   149→- Idempotent installation (second install returns count=0)
   150→- Force mode removes existing hooks
   151→- Preserves unknown fields in settings file
   152→- Uninstall removes only Entire hooks
   153→- localDev mode uses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR`
   154→
   155→### transcript_test.go
   156→
   157→- Extract modified files from JSONL
   158→- Extract prompts and summary
   159→- Token usage calculation
   160→- Chunking and reassembly
   161→
   162→All tests use `t.Parallel()`.
   163→

HookNames() []string {\n\treturn f.GetHookNames()\n}\n\n// ParseHookEvent translates a Factory AI Droid hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.\n// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase HookNameSessionStart:\n\t\treturn f.parseSessionStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameUserPromptSubmit:\n\t\treturn f.parseTurnStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameStop:\n\t\treturn f.parseTurnEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSessionEnd:\n\t\treturn f.parseSessionEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePreToolUse:\n\t\treturn f.parseSubagentStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePostToolUse:\n\t\treturn f.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePreCompact:\n\t\treturn f.parseCompaction(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSubagentStop, HookNameNotification:\n\t\t// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- TranscriptAnalyzer ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\treturn transcript.LineCount(path)\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {\n\tlines, currentPos, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startOffset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfiles := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)\n\treturn files, currentPos, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tlines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor i := range lines {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcontent := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)\n\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)\n\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", parseErr)\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg transcript.AssistantMessage\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn block.Text, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n// --- TranscriptPreparer ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {\n\twaitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// --- TokenCalculator ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\treturn CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, \"\")\n}\n\n// --- SubagentAwareExtractor ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {\n\treturn ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\treturn CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)\n}\n\n// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tPrompt:     raw.Prompt,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tevent := &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}\n\tif raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\tevent.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID\n\t}\n\treturn event, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[preCompactRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.Compaction,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---\n\nconst stopHookSentinel = \"hooks factoryai-droid stop\"\n\nfunc waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {\n\tconst (\n\t\tmaxWait      = 3 * time.Second\n\t\tpollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond\n\t\ttailBytes    = 4096\n\t\tmaxSkew      = 2 * time.Second\n\t)\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"agent.factoryaidroid\")\n\tdeadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)\n\tfor time.Now().Before(deadline) {\n\t\tif checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel found\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.Duration(\"wait\", time.Since(hookStartTime)),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t}\n\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding\",\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"timeout\", maxWait),\n\t)\n}\n\nfunc checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {\n\tf, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tdefer f.Close()\n\n\tinfo, err := f.Stat()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\toffset := info.Size() - tailBytes\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\tbuf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)\n\tif _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(string(buf), \"\\n\")\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\tline = strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif line == \"\" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar entry struct {\n\t\t\tTimestamp string `json:\"timestamp\"`\n\t\t}\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tlowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)\n\t\tupperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)\n\t\tif ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n```\n\n**Note:** This file references `ExtractModifiedFiles`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsage`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript`, and `ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript` which will be created in Task 5 (transcript.go). It also references `transcript.LineCount` — verify this function exists; if not, use an equivalent.\n\n**Step 4: Run the tests**\n\nRun: `cd /Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli && go test ./cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/ -run TestParseHookEvent -count=1 -v`\nExpected: PASS (all 12 tests)\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle.go cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle_test.go\ngit commit -m \"feat(factoryai-droid): add lifecycle event parsing with tests\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 4: Create hooks.go — Hook Installation\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks.go`\n- Create: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks_test.go`\n\n**Context:** Hook installation reads/writes `.factory/settings.json` at the repo root. Key differences:\n- Config dir is `.factory/` not `.claude/`\n- Env var is `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` not `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`\n- 7 hooks installed (adds PreCompact vs Claude Code's 7 that include PostTodo)\n- No PostTodo hook (that's Claude-specific)\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing hooks test**\n\nCreate `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks_test.go`:\n\n```go\npackage factoryaidroid\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_CreatesSettingsFile(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tfactoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".factory\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tt.Setenv(\"GIT_DIR\", filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"))\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n\t// Override repo root for testing\n\torigDir, _ := os.Getwd()\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck\n\n\tcount, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count != 7 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 7 hooks installed, got %d\", count)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify file exists and is valid JSON\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, \"settings.json\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to read settings.json: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar settings map[string]json.RawMessage\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"invalid JSON in settings.json: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify hooks key exists\n\tif _, ok := settings[\"hooks\"]; !ok {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected 'hooks' key in settings.json\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tfactoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".factory\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n\torigDir, _ := os.Getwd()\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck\n\n\t// First install\n\t_, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first install failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Second install should return 0\n\tcount, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"second install failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif count != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 0 new hooks on second install, got %d\", count)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownFields(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tfactoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".factory\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write settings with custom field\n\texisting := `{\"model\": \"opus\", \"hooks\": {}}`\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, \"settings.json\"), []byte(existing), 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n\torigDir, _ := os.Getwd()\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck\n\n\t_, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"install failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, \"settings.json\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar settings map[string]json.RawMessage\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify custom field preserved\n\tif _, ok := settings[\"model\"]; !ok {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected 'model' field to be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUninstallHooks_RemovesEntireHooks(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tfactoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".factory\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n\torigDir, _ := os.Getwd()\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck\n\n\t// Install first\n\t_, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"install failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Then uninstall\n\tif err := ag.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"uninstall failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify hooks are gone\n\tif ag.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected hooks to be uninstalled\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_LocalDev(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tfactoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".factory\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, \".git\"), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n\torigDir, _ := os.Getwd()\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck\n\n\t_, err := ag.InstallHooks(true, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"install failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, \"settings.json\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify localDev commands use FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR\n\tif !contains(string(data), \"${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"expected localDev commands to use ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc contains(s, substr string) bool {\n\treturn len(s) >= len(substr) && (s == substr || len(s) > 0 && containsSubstring(s, substr))\n}\n\nfunc containsSubstring(s, substr string) bool {\n\tfor i := 0; i <= len(s)-len(substr); i++ {\n\t\tif s[i:i+len(substr)] == substr {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n```\n\n**Note:** These tests use `os.Chdir` which means they cannot use `t.Parallel()` within the test itself. `★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Key architectural decisions in this plan:**
1. **Factory Droid reuses the `transcript` package** — Since both use JSONL, we avoid duplicating parsing logic. If the schema diverges later, we'd create a Factory-specific parser.
2. **9 hooks registered but only 7 installed** — SubagentStop and Notification are pass-throughs. We still register them as CLI subcommands (so Factory Droid can call them), but don't install them in the config (no framework action needed).
3. **PreCompact → Compaction** — This is a lifecycle event that Claude Code doesn't use but Gemini does. Factory Droid becomes the second agent using it, which validates the framework's event type design.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Some hooks add extra fields (`prompt`, `tool_name`, `tool_input`, `tool_response`, `stop_hook_active`). Study `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/types.go` for the pattern.

    22→**Step 1: Create the types file**
    23→
    24→```go
    25→package factoryaidroid
    26→
    27→import "encoding/json"
    28→
    29→// FactorySettings represents the .factory/settings.json structure.
    30→type FactorySettings struct {
    31→	Hooks FactoryHooks `json:"hooks"`
    32→}
    33→
    34→// FactoryHooks contains the hook configurations.
    35→type FactoryHooks struct {
    36→	SessionStart     []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    37→	SessionEnd       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    38→	UserPromptSubmit []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"UserPromptSubmit,omitempty"`
    39→	Stop             []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"Stop,omitempty"`
    40→	PreToolUse       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"PreToolUse,omitempty"`
    41→	PostToolUse      []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"PostToolUse,omitempty"`
    42→	PreCompact       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:"PreCompact,omitempty"`
    43→}
    44→
    45→// FactoryHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns.
    46→type FactoryHookMatcher struct {
    47→	Matcher string             `json:"matcher"`
    48→	Hooks   []FactoryHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    49→}
    50→
    51→// FactoryHookEntry represents a single hook command.
    52→type FactoryHookEntry struct {
    53→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    54→	Command string `json:"command"`
    55→}
    56→
    57→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd/Stop/SubagentStop hooks.
    58→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    59→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    60→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    61→}
    62→
    63→// userPromptSubmitRaw is the JSON structure from UserPromptSubmit hooks.
    64→type userPromptSubmitRaw struct {
    65→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    66→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    67→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
    68→}
    69→
    70→// preCompactRaw is the JSON structure from PreCompact hooks.
    71→type preCompactRaw struct {
    72→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    73→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    74→}
    75→
    76→// taskHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PreToolUse[Task] hook.
    77→type taskHookInputRaw struct {
    78→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`

    83→

   141→**Files:**

   143→
   144→**Context:** This is the core agent implementation with identity methods, transcript storage, and legacy methods. Study `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go` for the pattern. Key difference: Factory Droid uses `.factory/` dir, `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var, and JSONL transcripts (like Claude Code, unlike Gemini's JSON).
   145→
   146→The `init()` function registers the agent with the global registry. The import path must be added to `hooks_cmd.go` and `config.go` later (Task 8).
   147→
   148→**Step 1: Create the core agent file**
   149→
   150→```go
   151→package factoryaidroid
   152→
   153→import (
   154→	"errors"
   155→	"fmt"
   156→	"io"
   157→	"os"
   158→	"path/filepath"
   159→
   160→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   161→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
   162→)
   163→
   164→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
   165→func init() {
   166→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameFactoryAIDroid, NewFactoryAIDroidAgent)
   167→}
   168→
   169→// FactoryAIDroidAgent implements the agent.Agent interface for Factory AI Droid.
   170→type FactoryAIDroidAgent struct{}
   171→
   172→// NewFactoryAIDroidAgent creates a new Factory AI Droid agent instance.
   173→func NewFactoryAIDroidAgent() agent.Agent {
   174→	return &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
   175→}
   176→
   177→// --- Identity ---
   178→
   179→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Name() agent.AgentName  { return agent.AgentNameFactoryAIDroid }
   180→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return agent.AgentTypeFactoryAIDroid }
   181→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Description() string     { return "Factory AI Droid - agent-native development platform" }
   182→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".factory"} }
   183→
   184→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
   185→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		repoRoot = "."
   188→	}
   189→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".factory")); err == nil {
   190→		return true, nil
   191→	}
   192→	return false, nil
   193→}
   194→
   195→// --- Transcript Storage ---
   196→
   197→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
   198→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→	return data, nil
   203→}
   204→
   205→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   206→	return agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   207→}
   208→
   209→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   210→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   211→}
   212→
   213→// --- Legacy methods ---
   214→
   215→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string { return ".factory/settings.json" }
   216→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) SupportsHooks() bool       { return true }
   217→
   218→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
   219→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](r)
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil, err
   222→	}
   223→	return &agent.HookInput{
   224→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   225→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   226→	}, nil
   227→}
   228→
   229→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string { return input.SessionID }
   230→
   231→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   232→	return "", errors.New("not implemented")
   233→}
   234→
   235→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   236→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   237→}
   238→
   239→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   240→	return nil, errors.New("not implemented")
   241→}
   242→
   243→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {
   244→	return errors.New("not implemented")
   245→}
   246→
   247→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   248→	return "droid --session-id " + sessionID
   249→}
   250→```

   280→### Task 3: Create lifecycle.go — Hook Event Parsing
   281→
   282→**Files:**
   283→- Create: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle.go`
   284→
   285→**Context:** This is the core contribution surface — `ParseHookEvent` translates Factory Droid's 9 native hooks to normalized lifecycle events. Study `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle.go` for the Claude Code pattern and `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle.go:147-158` for the `parseCompaction` pattern (Gemini has this, Claude Code doesn't).
   286→
   287→**Step 1: Write the failing lifecycle test**
   288→
   289→Create `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle_test.go`:
   290→
   291→```go
   292→package factoryaidroid
   293→
   294→import (
   295→	"strings"
   296→	"testing"
   297→
   298→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   299→)
   300→
   301→func TestParseHookEvent_SessionStart(t *testing.T) {
   302→	t.Parallel()
   303→
   304→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
   305→	input := `{"session_id": "test-session", "transcript_path": "/tmp/transcript.jsonl"}`
   306→
   307→	event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameSessionStart, strings.NewReader(input))
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   310→	}
   311→	if event == nil {
   312→		t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   313→	}
   314→	if event.Type != agent.SessionStart {
   315→		t.Errorf("expected SessionStart, got %v", event.Type)
   316→	}
   317→	if event.SessionID != "test-session" {
   318→		t.Errorf("expected session_id 'test-session', got %q", event.SessionID)
   319→	}
   320→	if event.SessionRef != "/tmp/transcript.jsonl" {
   321→		t.Errorf("expected transcript_path '/tmp/transcript.jsonl', got %q", event.SessionRef)
   322→	}
   323→}
   324→
   325→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnStart(t *testing.T) {
   326→	t.Parallel()
   327→
   328→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
   329→	input := `{"session_id": "sess-1", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl", "prompt": "Fix the bug"}`
   330→
   331→	event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameUserPromptSubmit, strings.NewReader(input))
   332→	if err != nil {
   333→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   334→	}
   335→	if event.Type != agent.TurnStart {
   336→		t.Errorf("expected TurnStart, got %v", event.Type)
   337→	}
   338→	if event.Prompt != "Fix the bug" {
   339→		t.Errorf("expected prompt 'Fix the bug', got %q", event.Prompt)
   340→	}
   341→}
   342→
   343→func TestParseHookEvent_TurnEnd(t *testing.T) {
   344→	t.Parallel()
   345→
   346→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
   347→	input := `{"session_id": "sess-2", "transcript_path": "/tmp/t.jsonl"}`
   348→
   349→	event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(HookNameStop, strings.NewReader(input))
   350→	if err != nil {
   351→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   352→	}
   353→	if event.Type != agent.TurnEnd {

   489→**Step 3: Write lifecycle.go**

   491→```go
   492→package factoryaidroid
   493→
   494→import (
   495→	"context"
   496→	"encoding/json"
   497→	"fmt"
   498→	"io"
   499→	"log/slog"
   500→	"os"
   501→	"strings"
   502→	"time"
   503→
   504→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
   505→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
   506→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil"
   507→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
   508→)
   509→
   510→// Compile-time interface assertions.
   511→var (
   512→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer     = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)
   513→	_ agent.TranscriptPreparer     = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)
   514→	_ agent.TokenCalculator        = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)
   515→	_ agent.SubagentAwareExtractor = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)
   516→)
   517→
   518→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Factory AI Droid supports.
   519→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) HookNames() []string {
   520→	return f.GetHookNames()
   521→}
   522→
   523→// ParseHookEvent translates a Factory AI Droid hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
   524→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
   525→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   526→	switch hookName {
   527→	case HookNameSessionStart:
   528→		return f.parseSessionStart(stdin)
   529→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
   530→		return f.parseTurnStart(stdin)
   531→	case HookNameStop:
   532→		return f.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
   533→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
   534→		return f.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
   535→	case HookNamePreToolUse:
   536→		return f.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
   537→	case HookNamePostToolUse:
   538→		return f.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
   539→	case HookNamePreCompact:
   540→		return f.parseCompaction(stdin)
   541→	case HookNameSubagentStop, HookNameNotification:
   542→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
   543→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
   544→	default:
   545→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
   546→	}
   547→}
   548→
   549→// --- TranscriptAnalyzer ---
   550→
   551→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   552→	return transcript.LineCount(path)
   553→}
   554→
   555→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
   556→	lines, currentPos, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startOffset)
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   559→	}
   560→	files := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)
   561→	return files, currentPos, nil
   562→}
   563→
   564→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
   565→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
   566→	if err != nil {
   567→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	var prompts []string
   571→	for i := range lines {
   572→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
   573→			continue
   574→		}
   575→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
   576→		if content != "" {
   577→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
   578→		}
   579→	}
   580→	return prompts, nil
   581→}
   582→
   583→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
   584→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
   585→	if err != nil {
   586→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   587→	}
   588→
   589→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   590→	if parseErr != nil {
   591→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   595→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   596→			continue
   597→		}
   598→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   599→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   600→			continue
   601→		}
   602→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   603→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   604→				return block.Text, nil
   605→			}
   606→		}
   607→	}
   608→	return "", nil
   609→}
   610→
   611→// --- TranscriptPreparer ---
   612→
   613→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
   614→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   615→	return nil
   616→}
   617→
   618→// --- TokenCalculator ---
   619→
   620→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   621→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   622→}
   623→
   624→// --- SubagentAwareExtractor ---
   625→
   626→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   627→	return ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   628→}
   629→
   630→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   631→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   632→}
   633→
   634→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   635→
   636→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   637→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   638→	if err != nil {
   639→		return nil, err
   640→	}
   641→	return &agent.Event{
   642→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   643→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   644→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   645→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   646→	}, nil
   647→}
   648→
   649→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   650→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)
   651→	if err != nil {
   652→		return nil, err
   653→	}
   654→	return &agent.Event{
   655→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   656→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   657→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   658→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   659→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   660→	}, nil
   661→}
   662→
   663→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   664→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   665→	if err != nil {
   666→		return nil, err
   667→	}
   668→	return &agent.Event{
   669→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   670→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   671→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,

   673→	}, nil
   674→}
   675→
   676→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   677→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   678→	if err != nil {
   679→		return nil, err
   680→	}
   681→	return &agent.Event{
   682→		Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   683→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,

   688→
   689→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   690→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)
   691→	if err != nil {
   692→		return nil, err
   693→	}
   694→	return &agent.Event{
   695→		Type:       agent.SubagentStart,
   696→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   697→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   698→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   699→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,

   701→	}, nil
   702→}
   703→
   704→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   705→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   706→	if err != nil {
   707→		return nil, err
   708→	}
   709→	event := &agent.Event{
   710→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   711→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   712→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   713→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   714→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   715→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   716→	}
   717→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   718→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   719→	}
   720→	return event, nil
   721→}
   722→
   723→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   724→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[preCompactRaw](stdin)
   725→	if err != nil {
   726→		return nil, err
   727→	}
   728→	return &agent.Event{
   729→		Type:       agent.Compaction,
   730→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   731→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   732→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   733→	}, nil
   734→}
   735→
   736→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   737→
   738→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks factoryai-droid stop"
   739→
   740→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   741→	const (
   742→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   743→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   744→		tailBytes    = 4096
   745→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   746→	)
   747→
   748→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.factoryaidroid")
   749→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   750→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   751→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   752→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   753→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   754→			)
   755→			return
   756→		}
   757→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   758→	}
   759→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   760→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   761→	)
   762→}
   763→
   764→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   765→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return false
   768→	}
   769→	defer f.Close()
   770→
   771→	info, err := f.Stat()
   772→	if err != nil {
   773→		return false
   774→	}
   775→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   776→	if offset < 0 {
   777→		offset = 0
   778→	}
   779→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   780→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   781→		return false
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   785→	for _, line := range lines {
   786→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   787→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   788→			continue
   789→		}
   790→
   791→		var entry struct {
   792→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   793→		}
   794→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   795→			continue
   796→		}
   797→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   798→		if err != nil {
   799→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   800→			if err != nil {
   801→				continue
   802→			}
   803→		}
   804→		lowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   805→		upperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)
   806→		if ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {
   807→			return true
   808→		}
   809→	}
   810→	return false
   811→}
   812→```
   813→

   836→**Context:** Hook installation reads/writes `.factory/settings.json` at the repo root. The JSON format is identical to Claude Code's (no `hooksConfig.enabled` like Gemini). Study `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go` for the full pattern. Key differences:

   838→- Env var is `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` not `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`
   839→- 7 hooks installed (adds PreCompact vs Claude Code's 7 that include PostTodo)
   840→- No PostTodo hook (that's Claude-specific)

   902→func TestInstallHooks_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
   903→	t.Parallel()
   904→
   905→	dir := t.TempDir()
   906→	factoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".factory")
   907→	if err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {

   914→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}

   919→	defer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck
   920→
   921→	// First install
   922→	_, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   923→	if err != nil {
   924→		t.Fatalf("first install failed: %v", err)
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	// Second install should return 0
   928→	count, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   929→	if err != nil {
   930→		t.Fatalf("second install failed: %v", err)
   931→	}
   932→	if count != 0 {
   933→		t.Errorf("expected 0 new hooks on second install, got %d", count)
   934→	}
   935→}
   936→
   937→func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownFields(t *testing.T) {
   938→	t.Parallel()
   939→
   940→	dir := t.TempDir()
   941→	factoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".factory")
   942→	if err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   943→		t.Fatal(err)
   944→	}
   945→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, ".git"), 0o750); err != nil {
   946→		t.Fatal(err)
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	// Write settings with custom field
   950→	existing := `{"model": "opus", "hooks": {}}`
   951→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, "settings.json"), []byte(existing), 0o600); err != nil {
   952→		t.Fatal(err)
   953→	}
   954→
   955→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
   956→	origDir, _ := os.Getwd()
   957→	if err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {
   958→		t.Fatal(err)
   959→	}
   960→	defer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck
   961→
   962→	_, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
   963→	if err != nil {
   964→		t.Fatalf("install failed: %v", err)
   965→	}
   966→
   967→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, "settings.json"))
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		t.Fatal(err)
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	var settings map[string]json.RawMessage
   973→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {

   975→	}

   977→	// Verify custom field preserved

   984→	t.Parallel()
   985→
   986→	dir := t.TempDir()

  1019→func TestInstallHooks_LocalDev(t *testing.T) {
  1020→	t.Parallel()
  1021→
  1022→	dir := t.TempDir()
  1023→	factoryDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".factory")
  1024→	if err := os.MkdirAll(factoryDir, 0o750); err != nil {
  1025→		t.Fatal(err)
  1026→	}
  1027→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, ".git"), 0o750); err != nil {
  1028→		t.Fatal(err)
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	ag := &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}
  1032→	origDir, _ := os.Getwd()
  1033→	if err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {
  1034→		t.Fatal(err)
  1035→	}
  1036→	defer os.Chdir(origDir) //nolint:errcheck
  1037→
  1038→	_, err := ag.InstallHooks(true, false)
  1039→	if err != nil {
  1040→		t.Fatalf("install failed: %v", err)
  1041→	}
  1042→
  1043→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(factoryDir, "settings.json"))
  1044→	if err != nil {
  1045→		t.Fatal(err)
  1046→	}
  1047→
  1048→	// Verify localDev commands use FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR
  1049→	if !contains(string(data), "${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}") {
  1050→		t.Error("expected localDev commands to use ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}")
  1051→	}
  1052→}
  1053→
  1054→func contains(s, substr string) bool {
  1055→	return len(s) >= len(substr) && (s == substr || len(s) > 0 && containsSubstring(s, substr))
  1056→}
  1057→
  1058→func containsSubstring(s, substr string) bool {
  1059→	for i := 0; i <= len(s)-len(substr); i++ {
  1060→		if s[i:i+len(substr)] == substr {
  1061→			return true
  1062→		}
  1063→	}
  1064→	return false
  1065→}
  1066→```
  1067→
  1068→**Note:** These tests use `os.Chdir` which means they cannot use `t.Parallel()` within the test itself. The top-level `t.Parallel()` is removed because Go panics on `t.Setenv` or `os.Chdir` after `t.Parallel()`. Adjust if the repo conventions allow non-parallel for chdir tests.

    13→// metadataDenyRuleTest is the rule that blocks Claude from reading Entire metadata
    14→const metadataDenyRuleTest = "Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"
    15→
    16→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_FreshInstall(t *testing.T) {
    17→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    18→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    19→
    20→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    21→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    27→
    28→	// Verify permissions.deny contains our rule
    29→	if !containsRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
    30→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain %q", perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest)
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
    35→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    36→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    37→
    38→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    39→	// First install
    40→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		t.Fatalf("first InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Second install
    46→	_, err = agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		t.Fatalf("second InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    52→
    53→	// Count occurrences of our rule
    54→	count := 0
    55→	for _, rule := range perms.Deny {
    56→		if rule == metadataDenyRuleTest {
    57→			count++
    58→		}
    59→	}
    60→	if count != 1 {
    61→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny contains %d copies of rule, want 1", count)
    62→	}
    63→}
    64→
    65→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUserRules(t *testing.T) {

    69→	// Create settings.json with existing user deny rule

     1→// Package testutil provides shared test utilities for agent packages.
     2→package testutil
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"testing"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// ReadRawHooks reads the raw hooks map from a settings file.
    12→// settingsDir is the directory name (e.g., ".claude" or ".gemini").
    13→func ReadRawHooks(t *testing.T, tempDir, settingsDir string) map[string]json.RawMessage {
    14→	t.Helper()
    15→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, settingsDir, "settings.json")
    16→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // Test utility, path constructed from test tempDir
    17→	if err != nil {
    18→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
    19→	}
    20→
    21→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    22→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    27→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    28→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
    29→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse hooks: %v", err)
    30→		}
    31→	}
    32→	return rawHooks
    33→}
    34→
    35→// GetKeys returns the keys of a map as a slice.
    36→func GetKeys(m map[string]json.RawMessage) []string {
    37→	keys := make([]string, 0, len(m))
    38→	for k := range m {
    39→		keys = append(keys, k)
    40→	}
    41→	return keys
    42→}

  "content": "package factoryaidroid\n\nimport \"encoding/json\"\n\n// FactorySettings represents the .factory/settings.json structure.\ntype FactorySettings struct {\n\tHooks FactoryHooks `json:\"hooks\"`\n}\n\n// FactoryHooks contains the hook configurations.\ntype FactoryHooks struct {\n\tSessionStart     []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"SessionStart,omitempty\"`\n\tSessionEnd       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"SessionEnd,omitempty\"`\n\tUserPromptSubmit []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"UserPromptSubmit,omitempty\"`\n\tStop             []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"Stop,omitempty\"`\n\tPreToolUse       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"PreToolUse,omitempty\"`\n\tPostToolUse      []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"PostToolUse,omitempty\"`\n\tPreCompact       []FactoryHookMatcher `json:\"PreCompact,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// FactoryHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns.\ntype FactoryHookMatcher struct {\n\tMatcher string             `json:\"matcher\"`\n\tHooks   []FactoryHookEntry `json:\"hooks\"`\n}\n\n// FactoryHookEntry represents a single hook command.\ntype FactoryHookEntry struct {\n\tType    string `json:\"type\"`\n\tCommand string `json:\"command\"`\n}\n\n// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd/Stop/SubagentStop hooks.\ntype sessionInfoRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n}\n\n// userPromptSubmitRaw is the JSON structure from UserPromptSubmit hooks.\ntype userPromptSubmitRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tPrompt         string `json:\"prompt\"`\n}\n\n// preCompactRaw is the JSON structure from PreCompact hooks.\ntype preCompactRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n}\n\n// taskHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PreToolUse[Task] hook.\ntype taskHookInputRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string          `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string          `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tToolUseID      string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input\"`\n}\n\n// postToolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PostToolUse[Task] hook.\ntype postToolHookInputRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string          `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string          `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tToolUseID      string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input\"`\n\tToolResponse   struct {\n\t\tAgentID string `json:\"agentId\"`\n\t} `json:\"tool_response\"`\n}\n\n// Tool names used in Factory Droid transcripts.\nconst (\n\tToolWrite        = \"Write\"\n\tToolEdit         = \"Edit\"\n\tToolNotebookEdit = \"NotebookEdit\"\n)\n\n// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files.\nvar FileModificationTools = []string{\n\tToolWrite,\n\tToolEdit,\n\tToolNotebookEdit,\n}\n\n// messageUsage represents token usage from an API response.\ntype messageUsage struct {\n\tInputTokens              int `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\tCacheCreationInputTokens int `json:\"cache_creation_input_tokens\"`\n\tCacheReadInputTokens     int `json:\"cache_read_input_tokens\"`\n\tOutputTokens             int `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n}\n\n// messageWithUsage represents an assistant message with usage data.\ntype messageWithUsage struct {\n\tID    string       `json:\"id\"`\n\tUsage messageUsage `json:\"usage\"`\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package factoryaidroid\n\nimport (\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern\nfunc init() {\n\tagent.Register(agent.AgentNameFactoryAIDroid, NewFactoryAIDroidAgent)\n}\n\n// FactoryAIDroidAgent implements the agent.Agent interface for Factory AI Droid.\ntype FactoryAIDroidAgent struct{}\n\n// NewFactoryAIDroidAgent creates a new Factory AI Droid agent instance.\nfunc NewFactoryAIDroidAgent() agent.Agent {\n\treturn &FactoryAIDroidAgent{}\n}\n\n// --- Identity ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Name() agent.AgentName  { return agent.AgentNameFactoryAIDroid }\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return agent.AgentTypeFactoryAIDroid }\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) Description() string     { return \"Factory AI Droid - agent-native development platform\" }\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{\".factory\"} }\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".factory\")); err == nil {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\treturn false, nil\n}\n\n// --- Transcript Storage ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {\n\treturn agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil\n}\n\n// --- Legacy methods ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string { return \".factory/settings.json\" }\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) SupportsHooks() bool       { return true }\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookInput(_ agent.HookType, r io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](r)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.HookInput{\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string { return input.SessionID }\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {\n\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"not implemented\")\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+\".jsonl\")\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {\n\treturn nil, errors.New(\"not implemented\")\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {\n\treturn errors.New(\"not implemented\")\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {\n\treturn \"droid --session-id \" + sessionID\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle.go",
  "content": "package factoryaidroid\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript\"\n)\n\n// Compile-time interface assertions.\nvar (\n\t_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer     = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.TranscriptPreparer     = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.TokenCalculator        = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.SubagentAwareExtractor = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)\n)\n\n// HookNames returns the hook verbs Factory AI Droid supports.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) HookNames() []string {\n\treturn f.GetHookNames()\n}\n\n// ParseHookEvent translates a Factory AI Droid hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.\n// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase HookNameSessionStart:\n\t\treturn f.parseSessionStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameUserPromptSubmit:\n\t\treturn f.parseTurnStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameStop:\n\t\treturn f.parseTurnEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSessionEnd:\n\t\treturn f.parseSessionEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePreToolUse:\n\t\treturn f.parseSubagentStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePostToolUse:\n\t\treturn f.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePreCompact:\n\t\treturn f.parseCompaction(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSubagentStop, HookNameNotification:\n\t\t// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action\n\t}\n}\n\n// --- TranscriptAnalyzer ---\n\n// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\t_, pos, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err //nolint:wrapcheck // caller adds context\n\t}\n\treturn pos, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {\n\tlines, currentPos, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startOffset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfiles := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)\n\treturn files, currentPos, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tlines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor i := range lines {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcontent := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)\n\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)\n\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", parseErr)\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg transcript.AssistantMessage\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn block.Text, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n// --- TranscriptPreparer ---\n\n// PrepareTranscript waits for Factory Droid's async transcript flush to complete.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {\n\twaitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// --- TokenCalculator ---\n\n// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\treturn CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, \"\")\n}\n\n// --- SubagentAwareExtractor ---\n\n// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {\n\treturn ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)\n}\n\n// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\treturn CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)\n}\n\n// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tPrompt:     raw.Prompt,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tevent := &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}\n\tif raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\tevent.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID\n\t}\n\treturn event, nil\n}\n\nfunc (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[preCompactRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.Compaction,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.SessionID,\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---\n\nconst stopHookSentinel = \"hooks factoryai-droid stop\"\n\nfunc waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {\n\tconst (\n\t\tmaxWait      = 3 * time.Second\n\t\tpollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond\n\t\ttailBytes    = 4096\n\t\tmaxSkew      = 2 * time.Second\n\t)\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"agent.factoryaidroid\")\n\tdeadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)\n\tfor time.Now().Before(deadline) {\n\t\tif checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel found\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.Duration(\"wait\", time.Since(hookStartTime)),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t}\n\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding\",\n\t\tslog.Duration(\"timeout\", maxWait),\n\t)\n}\n\nfunc checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {\n\tfile, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\tinfo, err := file.Stat()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\toffset := info.Size() - tailBytes\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\tbuf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)\n\tif _, err := file.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(string(buf), \"\\n\")\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\tline = strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif line == \"\" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar entry struct {\n\t\t\tTimestamp string `json:\"timestamp\"`\n\t\t}\n\t\tif json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tlowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)\n\t\tupperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)\n\t\tif ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n"
}

The file compiles without errors. Here is what was created:

**File:** `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/transcript.go`

**Functions provided:**

1. **`ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string`** -- Extracts file paths from JSONL `tool_use` blocks, checking against `FileModificationTools` (Write, Edit, NotebookEdit) from types.go. Returns a deduplicated ordered list.

2. **`CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage`** -- Sums token usage from assistant messages, deduplicating by message ID (taking the entry with the highest `output_tokens` to handle streaming duplicates).

3. **`CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error)`** -- File-based wrapper that parses from a given line offset and delegates to `CalculateTokenUsage`.

4. **`CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error)`** -- The function called by lifecycle.go's `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` method. Parses the main transcript once, calculates main usage, extracts spawned agent IDs, and accumulates subagent token usage into `SubagentTokens`.

5. **`ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)`** -- The function called by lifecycle.go's `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` method. Parses the main transcript once, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each subagent transcript and merges their modified files into a deduplicated list.

6. **`ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) map[string]string`** -- Finds subagent IDs in `tool_result` blocks by looking for "agentId: <id>" patterns. Returns a map of agentID to toolUseID.

7. **`SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error)`** -- Converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes.

**Type aliases:**
- `TranscriptLine = transcript.Line`
- `userMessage = transcript.UserMessage`
- `assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage`
- `toolInput = transcript.ToolInput`

**Key differences from claudecode/transcript.go:**
- Function names `CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript` and `ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript` match what lifecycle.go expects (vs `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` and `ExtractAllModifiedFiles` in the Claude Code package).
- Omits `ExtractLastUserPrompt`, `TruncateAtUUID`, and `FindCheckpointUUID` which are not referenced in the factoryaidroid package.

The file compiles and passes `go vet` cleanly. Here is a summary of what was created.

**File created:** `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks.go`

**What it implements:**

The file follows the exact pattern from the Claude Code `hooks.go` but adapted for Factory AI Droid. Key adaptations:

1. **Package and types**: Uses `factoryaidroid` package with `FactoryAIDroidAgent`, `FactoryHookMatcher`, and `FactoryHookEntry` types (already defined in `types.go`).

2. **Compile-time assertions**: `_ agent.HookSupport = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)` and `_ agent.HookHandler = (*FactoryAIDroidAgent)(nil)`.

3. **Hook name constants**: All 9 hooks defined -- `session-start`, `session-end`, `stop`, `user-prompt-submit`, `pre-tool-use`, `post-tool-use`, `subagent-stop`, `pre-compact`, `notification`.

4. **`entireHookPrefixes`**: Uses `FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR` env var instead of `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`.

5. **Config directory**: `.factory/` instead of `.claude/`, with `settings.json`.

6. **`InstallHooks`**: Installs 7 hooks (SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task], PreCompact). Uses `factoryai-droid` as the agent name in commands. Also handles `permissions.deny` with the `metadataDenyRule`.

7. **`UninstallHooks`**: Removes all Entire hooks from all 7 hook types plus the PreCompact hook type, and cleans up `permissions.deny`.

8. **`AreHooksInstalled`**: Checks for the Stop hook in both production and localDev formats.

9. **`GetSupportedHooks`**: Returns the 6 standard `agent.HookType` values.
