   251→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   252→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   253→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]FactoryHookMatcher) {
   254→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   255→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   256→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   257→	}
   258→}
   259→
   260→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   261→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   262→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) {
   263→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   264→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   265→		return
   266→	}
   267→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   270→	}
   271→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   272→}
   273→
   274→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Factory AI Droid settings.
   275→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   276→	// Use repo root to find .factory directory when run from a subdirectory
   277→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   280→	}
   281→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".factory", FactorySettingsFileName)
   282→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   288→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
   293→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   294→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   295→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   296→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   297→		}
   298→	}
   299→	if rawHooks == nil {
   300→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   304→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse, preCompact []FactoryHookMatcher
   305→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   306→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   307→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   308→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   309→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   310→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   311→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompact", &preCompact)
   312→
   313→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   314→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   315→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   316→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   317→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   318→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   319→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   320→	preCompact = removeEntireHooks(preCompact)
   321→
   322→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   323→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   324→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   325→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   326→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   327→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   328→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   329→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompact", preCompact)
   330→
   331→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   332→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   333→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   334→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   335→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   336→			rawPermissions = nil
   337→		}
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   341→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   342→			var denyRules []string
   343→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   344→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   345→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   346→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   347→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   348→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   349→					}
   350→				}
   351→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   352→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   353→					if err == nil {
   354→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   355→					}
   356→				} else {
   357→					// Remove empty deny array
   358→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   359→				}
   360→			}
   361→		}
   362→
   363→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   364→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   365→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   366→			if err == nil {
   367→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   368→			}
   369→		} else {
   370→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   371→		}
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   375→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   376→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   377→		if err != nil {
   378→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   379→		}
   380→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   381→	} else {
   382→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Write back
   386→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   387→	if err != nil {
   388→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   389→	}
   390→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   391→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   392→	}
   393→	return nil
   394→}
   395→
   396→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   397→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   398→	// Use repo root to find .factory directory when run from a subdirectory
   399→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   400→	if err != nil {
   401→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   402→	}
   403→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".factory", FactorySettingsFileName)
   404→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   405→	if err != nil {
   406→		return false
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	var settings FactorySettings
   410→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   411→		return false
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   415→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks factoryai-droid stop") ||
   416→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid stop")
   417→}
   418→
   419→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Factory AI Droid supports.
   420→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   421→	return []agent.HookType{
   422→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   423→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   424→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   425→		agent.HookStop,
   426→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   427→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   428→	}
   429→}
   430→
   431→// Helper functions for hook management
   432→
   433→func hookCommandExists(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   434→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   435→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   436→			if hook.Command == command {
   437→				return true
   438→			}
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return false
   442→}
   443→
   444→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   445→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   446→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   447→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   448→				if hook.Command == command {
   449→					return true
   450→				}
   451→			}
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→	return false
   455→}
   456→
   457→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   458→	entry := FactoryHookEntry{
   459→		Type:    "command",
   460→		Command: command,
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   464→	if matcherName == "" {
   465→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   466→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   467→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   468→				return matchers
   469→			}
   470→		}
   471→		return append(matchers, FactoryHookMatcher{
   472→			Matcher: "",
   473→			Hooks:   []FactoryHookEntry{entry},
   474→		})
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   478→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   479→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   480→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   481→			return matchers
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	return append(matchers, FactoryHookMatcher{
   486→		Matcher: matcherName,
   487→		Hooks:   []FactoryHookEntry{entry},
   488→	})
   489→}
   490→
   491→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   492→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   493→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   494→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   495→			return true
   496→		}
   497→	}
   498→	return false
   499→}
   500→
   501→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   502→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   503→	result := make([]FactoryHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   504→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   505→		filteredHooks := make([]FactoryHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   506→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   507→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   508→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   509→			}
   510→		}
   511→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   512→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   513→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   514→			result = append(result, matcher)
   515→		}
   516→	}
   517→	return result
   518→}
   519→
   520→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   521→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task")
   522→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   523→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   524→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   525→}
   526→
```

     1→package factoryaidroid
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    15→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    16→
    17→// Type aliases for internal use.
    18→type (
    19→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    20→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
    21→	toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
    22→)
    23→
    24→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes.
    25→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    26→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    27→	for _, line := range lines {
    28→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    29→		if err != nil {
    30→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    31→		}
    32→		buf.Write(data)
    33→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    34→	}
    35→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    36→}
    37→
    38→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript.
    39→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    40→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    41→	var files []string
    42→
    43→	for _, line := range lines {
    44→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    45→			continue
    46→		}
    47→
    48→		var msg assistantMessage
    49→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    50→			continue
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    54→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    55→				continue
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    59→			isModifyTool := false
    60→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    61→				if block.Name == name {
    62→					isModifyTool = true
    63→					break
    64→				}
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			if !isModifyTool {
    68→				continue
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			var input toolInput
    72→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    73→				continue
    74→			}
    75→
    76→			file := input.FilePath
    77→			if file == "" {
    78→				file = input.NotebookPath
    79→			}
    80→
    81→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
    82→				fileSet[file] = true
    83→				files = append(files, file)
    84→			}
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	return files
    89→}
    90→
    91→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Factory AI Droid transcript.
    92→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
    93→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
    94→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
    95→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
    96→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
    97→
    98→	for _, line := range transcriptLines {
    99→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   100→			continue
   101→		}
   102→
   103→		var msg messageWithUsage
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→
   108→		if msg.ID == "" {
   109→			continue
   110→		}
   111→
   112→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   113→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   114→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   115→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   116→		}
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Sum up all unique messages
   120→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   121→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   122→	}
   123→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   124→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   125→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   126→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   127→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	return usage
   131→}
   132→
   133→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a transcript file.
   134→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   135→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   136→	if path == "" {
   137→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // caller adds context
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return CalculateTokenUsage(lines), nil
   146→}
   147→
   148→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   149→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   150→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   151→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   152→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   153→
   154→	for _, line := range transcriptLines {
   155→		if line.Type != "user" {
   156→			continue
   157→		}
   158→
   159→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   160→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   161→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   162→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   163→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   164→		}
   165→
   166→		var msg struct {
   167→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   168→		}
   169→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   170→			continue
   171→		}
   172→
   173→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   174→			continue
   175→		}
   176→
   177→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   178→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   179→				continue
   180→			}
   181→
   182→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   183→			var textContent string
   184→
   185→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   186→			var textBlocks []struct {
   187→				Type string `json:"type"`
   188→				Text string `json:"text"`
   189→			}
   190→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   191→				var sb strings.Builder
   192→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   193→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   194→						sb.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   195→					}
   196→				}
   197→				textContent += sb.String()
   198→			} else {
   199→				// Try as plain string
   200→				var str string
   201→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   202→					textContent = str
   203→				}
   204→			}
   205→
   206→			// Look for agentId in the text
   207→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   208→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   209→			}
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	return agentIDs
   214→}
   215→
   216→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   217→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   218→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   219→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   220→	if idx == -1 {
   221→		return ""
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   225→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   226→	end := start
   227→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   228→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   229→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   230→		end++
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	if end > start {
   234→		return text[start:end]
   235→	}
   236→	return ""
   237→}
   238→
   239→// CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   240→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   241→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   242→func CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   243→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   244→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Parse transcript once
   248→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   254→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(parsed)
   255→
   256→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   257→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   258→
   259→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   260→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   261→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   262→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   263→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   264→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   265→			if err != nil {
   266→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   267→				continue
   268→			}
   269→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   270→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   271→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   272→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   273→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   274→		}
   275→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   276→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   277→		}
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	return mainUsage, nil
   281→}
   282→
   283→// ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   284→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   285→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   286→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   287→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   288→func ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   289→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   290→		return nil, nil
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	// Parse main transcript once
   294→	parsed, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   295→	if err != nil {
   296→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Collect modified files from main agent
   300→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   301→	var files []string
   302→	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(parsed) {
   303→		if !fileSet[f] {
   304→			fileSet[f] = true
   305→			files = append(files, f)
   306→		}
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   310→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   311→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   312→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   313→		agentLines, _, agentErr := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(agentPath, 0)
   314→		if agentErr != nil {
   315→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   316→			continue
   317→		}
   318→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentLines) {
   319→			if !fileSet[f] {
   320→				fileSet[f] = true
   321→				files = append(files, f)
   322→			}
   323→		}
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	return files, nil
   327→}
   328→

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"os"
     6→	"path/filepath"
     7→	"slices"
     8→	"testing"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/testutil"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// metadataDenyRuleTest is the rule that blocks Claude from reading Entire metadata
    14→const metadataDenyRuleTest = "Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"
    15→
    16→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_FreshInstall(t *testing.T) {
    17→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    18→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    19→
    20→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    21→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    27→
    28→	// Verify permissions.deny contains our rule
    29→	if !containsRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
    30→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain %q", perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest)
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
    35→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    36→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    37→
    38→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    39→	// First install
    40→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		t.Fatalf("first InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Second install
    46→	_, err = agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		t.Fatalf("second InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    52→
    53→	// Count occurrences of our rule
    54→	count := 0
    55→	for _, rule := range perms.Deny {
    56→		if rule == metadataDenyRuleTest {
    57→			count++
    58→		}
    59→	}
    60→	if count != 1 {
    61→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny contains %d copies of rule, want 1", count)
    62→	}
    63→}
    64→
    65→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUserRules(t *testing.T) {
    66→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    67→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    68→
    69→	// Create settings.json with existing user deny rule
    70→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
    71→  "permissions": {
    72→    "deny": ["Bash(rm -rf *)"]
    73→  }
    74→}`)
    75→
    76→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    77→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    78→	if err != nil {
    79→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    83→
    84→	// Verify both rules exist
    85→	if !containsRule(perms.Deny, "Bash(rm -rf *)") {
    86→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain user rule", perms.Deny)
    87→	}
    88→	if !containsRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
    89→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain Entire rule", perms.Deny)
    90→	}
    91→}
    92→
    93→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesAllowRules(t *testing.T) {
    94→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    95→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    96→
    97→	// Create settings.json with existing allow rules
    98→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
    99→  "permissions": {
   100→    "allow": ["Read(**)", "Write(**)"]
   101→  }
   102→}`)
   103→
   104→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   105→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
   111→
   112→	// Verify allow rules are preserved
   113→	if len(perms.Allow) != 2 {
   114→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want 2 rules", perms.Allow)
   115→	}
   116→	if !containsRule(perms.Allow, "Read(**)") {
   117→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want to contain Read(**)", perms.Allow)
   118→	}
   119→	if !containsRule(perms.Allow, "Write(**)") {
   120→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want to contain Write(**)", perms.Allow)

   217→	}

   219→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want [Read(**)]", allowRules)
   220→	}

   223→// Helper functions
   224→
   225→// testPermissions is used only for test assertions
   226→type testPermissions struct {
   227→	Allow []string `json:"allow,omitempty"`
   228→	Deny  []string `json:"deny,omitempty"`
   229→}
   230→
   231→func readPermissions(t *testing.T, tempDir string) testPermissions {
   232→	t.Helper()
   233→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   234→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   240→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   241→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	var perms testPermissions
   245→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   246→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &perms); err != nil {
   247→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions: %v", err)
   248→		}
   249→	}
   250→	return perms
   251→}
   252→
   253→func writeSettingsFile(t *testing.T, tempDir, content string) {
   254→	t.Helper()
   255→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude")
   256→	if err := os.MkdirAll(claudeDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   257→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .claude dir: %v", err)
   258→	}
   259→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(claudeDir, "settings.json")
   260→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   261→		t.Fatalf("failed to write settings.json: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→}
   264→
   265→func containsRule(rules []string, rule string) bool {
   266→	return slices.Contains(rules, rule)
   267→}

   269→func TestUninstallHooks(t *testing.T) {
   270→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   271→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   272→
   273→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   274→
   275→	// First install
   276→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)

   278→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Verify hooks are installed
   282→	if !agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   283→		t.Error("hooks should be installed before uninstall")
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Uninstall
   287→	err = agent.UninstallHooks()
   288→	if err != nil {
   289→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Verify hooks are removed
   293→	if agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   294→		t.Error("hooks should not be installed after uninstall")
   295→	}
   296→}

   436→//nolint:tparallel // Parent uses t.Chdir() which prevents t.Parallel(); subtests only read from pre-loaded data
   437→func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType(t *testing.T) {
   438→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   439→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   440→
   441→	// Create settings with user hooks on the same hook types we use
   442→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   443→  "hooks": {
   444→    "Stop": [
   445→      {
   446→        "matcher": "",
   447→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user stop hook"}]
   448→      }
   449→    ],
   450→    "SessionStart": [
   451→      {
   452→        "matcher": "",
   453→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user session start"}]
   454→      }
   455→    ],
   456→    "PostToolUse": [
   457→      {
   458→        "matcher": "Write",
   459→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user wrote file"}]
   460→      }
   461→    ]
   462→  }
   463→}`)
   464→
   465→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   466→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   467→	if err != nil {
   468→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	rawHooks := testutil.ReadRawHooks(t, tempDir, ".claude")
   472→
   473→	t.Run("Stop", func(t *testing.T) {
   474→		t.Parallel()
   475→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   476→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["Stop"], &matchers); err != nil {
   477→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse Stop hooks: %v", err)
   478→		}
   479→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "echo user stop hook", "user Stop hook")
   480→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "entire hooks claude-code stop", "Entire Stop hook")
   481→	})
   482→
   483→	t.Run("SessionStart", func(t *testing.T) {
   484→		t.Parallel()
   485→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   486→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["SessionStart"], &matchers); err != nil {
   487→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse SessionStart hooks: %v", err)
   488→		}
   489→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "echo user session start", "user SessionStart hook")
   490→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "entire hooks claude-code session-start", "Entire SessionStart hook")
   491→	})
   492→
   493→	t.Run("PostToolUse", func(t *testing.T) {
   494→		t.Parallel()
   495→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   496→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["PostToolUse"], &matchers); err != nil {
   497→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse PostToolUse hooks: %v", err)
   498→		}
   499→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "Write", "echo user wrote file", "user Write hook")
   500→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "Task", "entire hooks claude-code post-task", "Entire Task hook")
   501→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "TodoWrite", "entire hooks claude-code post-todo", "Entire TodoWrite hook")
   502→	})
   503→}
   504→
   505→// assertHookExists checks that a hook with the given matcher and command exists
   506→func assertHookExists(t *testing.T, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcher, command, description string) {
   507→	t.Helper()
   508→	for _, m := range matchers {
   509→		if m.Matcher == matcher {
   510→			for _, h := range m.Hooks {
   511→				if h.Command == command {
   512→					return
   513→				}
   514→			}
   515→		}
   516→	}
   517→	t.Errorf("%s was not found (matcher=%q, command=%q)", description, matcher, command)
   518→}

   520→func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes(t *testing.T) {
   521→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   522→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   523→
   524→	// Create settings with a hook type we don't handle (Notification is a real Claude Code hook type)
   525→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   526→  "hooks": {
   527→    "Notification": [
   528→      {
   529→        "matcher": "",
   530→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo notification received"}]
   531→      }
   532→    ],
   533→    "SubagentStop": [
   534→      {
   535→        "matcher": ".*",
   536→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo subagent stopped"}]
   537→      }
   538→    ]
   539→  }
   540→}`)
   541→
   542→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   543→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	// Read raw settings to check for unknown hook types
   549→	rawHooks := testutil.ReadRawHooks(t, tempDir, ".claude")
   550→
   551→	// Verify Notification hook is preserved
   552→	if _, ok := rawHooks["Notification"]; !ok {
   553→		t.Errorf("Notification hook type was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawHooks))
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Verify SubagentStop hook is preserved
   557→	if _, ok := rawHooks["SubagentStop"]; !ok {
   558→		t.Errorf("SubagentStop hook type was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawHooks))
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Verify the Notification hook content is intact
   562→	var notificationMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   563→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["Notification"], &notificationMatchers); err != nil {
   564→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse Notification hooks: %v", err)
   565→	}
   566→	if len(notificationMatchers) != 1 {
   567→		t.Errorf("Notification matchers = %d, want 1", len(notificationMatchers))
   568→	}
   569→	if len(notificationMatchers) > 0 && len(notificationMatchers[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   570→		if notificationMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo notification received" {
   571→			t.Errorf("Notification hook command = %q, want %q",
   572→				notificationMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command, "echo notification received")
   573→		}
   574→	}
   575→
   576→	// Verify the SubagentStop hook content is intact
   577→	var subagentStopMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   578→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["SubagentStop"], &subagentStopMatchers); err != nil {
   579→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse SubagentStop hooks: %v", err)
   580→	}
   581→	if len(subagentStopMatchers) != 1 {
   582→		t.Errorf("SubagentStop matchers = %d, want 1", len(subagentStopMatchers))
   583→	}
   584→	if len(subagentStopMatchers) > 0 {
   585→		if subagentStopMatchers[0].Matcher != ".*" {
   586→			t.Errorf("SubagentStop matcher = %q, want %q", subagentStopMatchers[0].Matcher, ".*")
   587→		}
   588→		if len(subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   589→			if subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo subagent stopped" {
   590→				t.Errorf("SubagentStop hook command = %q, want %q",
   591→					subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command, "echo subagent stopped")
   592→			}
   593→		}
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Verify our hooks were also installed
   597→	if _, ok := rawHooks["Stop"]; !ok {
   598→		t.Errorf("Stop hook should have been installed")
   599→	}
   600→}

    10→
    11→// ReadRawHooks reads the raw hooks map from a settings file.
    12→// settingsDir is the directory name (e.g., ".claude" or ".gemini").
    13→func ReadRawHooks(t *testing.T, tempDir, settingsDir string) map[string]json.RawMessage {
    14→	t.Helper()
    15→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, settingsDir, "settings.json")
    16→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // Test utility, path constructed from test tempDir
    17→	if err != nil {
    18→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
    19→	}
    20→
    21→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    22→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    27→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    28→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
    29→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse hooks: %v", err)
    30→		}
    31→	}
    32→	return rawHooks
    33→}
    34→
    35→// GetKeys returns the keys of a map as a slice.
    36→func GetKeys(m map[string]json.RawMessage) []string {
    37→	keys := make([]string, 0, len(m))
    38→	for k := range m {
    39→		keys = append(keys, k)
    40→	}
    41→	return keys
    42→}
    43→
```

    12→func TestParseTranscript(t *testing.T) {
    13→	t.Parallel()
    14→
    15→	data := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"hello"}}
    16→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"hi"}]}}
    17→`)
    18→
    19→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    20→	if err != nil {
    21→		t.Fatalf("ParseFromBytes() error = %v", err)
    22→	}
    23→
    24→	if len(lines) != 2 {
    25→		t.Errorf("ParseFromBytes() got %d lines, want 2", len(lines))
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	if lines[0].Type != transcript.TypeUser || lines[0].UUID != "u1" {
    29→		t.Errorf("First line = %+v, want type=user, uuid=u1", lines[0])
    30→	}
    31→
    32→	if lines[1].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant || lines[1].UUID != "a1" {
    33→		t.Errorf("Second line = %+v, want type=assistant, uuid=a1", lines[1])
    34→	}
    35→}

    37→func TestParseTranscript_SkipsMalformed(t *testing.T) {

    41→not valid json

    45→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    46→	if err != nil {

    50→	// Should skip the malformed line

    80→func TestExtractModifiedFiles(t *testing.T) {

    84→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Edit","input":{"file_path":"bar.go"}}]}}
    85→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a3","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"ls"}}]}}
    86→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a4","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"foo.go"}}]}}
    87→`)
    88→
    89→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)

    93→	files := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)

    95→	// Should have foo.go and bar.go (deduplicated, Bash not included)
    96→	if len(files) != 2 {

    98→	}
    99→
   100→	hasFile := func(name string) bool {

   104→			}
   105→		}
   106→		return false
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	if !hasFile("foo.go") {
   110→		t.Error("ExtractModifiedFiles() missing foo.go")
   111→	}
   112→	if !hasFile("bar.go") {
   113→		t.Error("ExtractModifiedFiles() missing bar.go")
   114→	}
   115→}

   117→func TestExtractLastUserPrompt(t *testing.T) {
   118→	t.Parallel()
   119→
   120→	tests := []struct {
   121→		name string
   122→		data string
   123→		want string
   124→	}{
   125→		{
   126→			name: "string content",
   127→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first"}}
   128→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[]}}
   129→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second"}}`,
   130→			want: "second",
   131→		},
   132→		{
   133→			name: "array content with text block",
   134→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"hello world"}]}}`,
   135→			want: "hello world",
   136→		},
   137→		{
   138→			name: "empty transcript",
   139→			data: ``,
   140→			want: "",
   141→		},
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	for _, tt := range tests {
   145→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   146→			t.Parallel()
   147→			lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes([]byte(tt.data))
   148→			if err != nil && tt.data != "" {
   149→				t.Fatalf("ParseFromBytes() error = %v", err)
   150→			}
   151→			got := ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   152→			if got != tt.want {
   153→				t.Errorf("ExtractLastUserPrompt() = %q, want %q", got, tt.want)
   154→			}
   155→		})
   156→	}
   157→}

   159→func TestTruncateAtUUID(t *testing.T) {
   160→	t.Parallel()
   161→
   162→	data := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{}}
   163→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{}}
   164→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{}}
   165→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{}}
   166→`)
   167→
   168→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		t.Fatalf("ParseFromBytes() error = %v", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	tests := []struct {
   174→		name     string
   175→		uuid     string
   176→		wantLen  int
   177→		lastUUID string
   178→	}{
   179→		{"truncate at u1", "u1", 1, "u1"},
   180→		{"truncate at a1", "a1", 2, "a1"},
   181→		{"truncate at u2", "u2", 3, "u2"},
   182→		{"truncate at a2", "a2", 4, "a2"},
   183→		{"empty uuid returns all", "", 4, "a2"},
   184→		{"unknown uuid returns all", "unknown", 4, "a2"},
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	for _, tt := range tests {
   188→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   189→			t.Parallel()
   190→			truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, tt.uuid)
   191→			if len(truncated) != tt.wantLen {
   192→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) got %d lines, want %d", tt.uuid, len(truncated), tt.wantLen)
   193→			}
   194→			if len(truncated) > 0 && truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID != tt.lastUUID {
   195→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) last UUID = %q, want %q", tt.uuid, truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID, tt.lastUUID)
   196→			}
   197→		})
   198→	}
   199→}

   201→func TestFindCheckpointUUID(t *testing.T) {
   202→	t.Parallel()
   203→
   204→	data := []byte(`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool1"}]}}
   205→{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"tool1"}]}}
   206→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool2"}]}}

   214→
   215→	tests := []struct {
   216→		toolUseID string
   217→		wantUUID  string
   218→		wantFound bool
   219→	}{

   221→		{"tool2", "u2", true},

   225→	for _, tt := range tests {

   231→			}

   233→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID(%q) uuid = %q, want %q", tt.toolUseID, uuid, tt.wantUUID)
   234→			}
   235→		})
   236→	}
   237→}

   239→// Token calculation tests - Claude Code specific token format
   240→
   241→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_BasicMessages(t *testing.T) {
   242→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   243→		{
   244→			Type: "assistant",
   245→			UUID: "asst-1",
   246→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   247→				"id": "msg_001",
   248→				"usage": map[string]int{
   249→					"input_tokens":                10,
   250→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   251→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   252→					"output_tokens":               20,
   253→				},
   254→			}),
   255→		},
   256→		{
   257→			Type: "assistant",
   258→			UUID: "asst-2",
   259→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   260→				"id": "msg_002",
   261→				"usage": map[string]int{
   262→					"input_tokens":                5,
   263→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 200,
   264→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     0,
   265→					"output_tokens":               30,
   266→				},
   267→			}),
   268→		},
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   272→
   273→	if usage.APICallCount != 2 {
   274→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 2", usage.APICallCount)
   275→	}
   276→	if usage.InputTokens != 15 {
   277→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 15", usage.InputTokens)
   278→	}
   279→	if usage.CacheCreationTokens != 300 {
   280→		t.Errorf("CacheCreationTokens = %d, want 300", usage.CacheCreationTokens)
   281→	}
   282→	if usage.CacheReadTokens != 50 {
   283→		t.Errorf("CacheReadTokens = %d, want 50", usage.CacheReadTokens)
   284→	}
   285→	if usage.OutputTokens != 50 {
   286→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 50", usage.OutputTokens)
   287→	}
   288→}
   289→
   290→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_StreamingDeduplication(t *testing.T) {
   291→	// Simulate streaming: multiple rows with same message ID, increasing output_tokens
   292→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   293→		{
   294→			Type: "assistant",
   295→			UUID: "asst-1",
   296→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   297→				"id": "msg_001",
   298→				"usage": map[string]int{
   299→					"input_tokens":                10,
   300→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   301→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   302→					"output_tokens":               1, // First streaming chunk
   303→				},
   304→			}),
   305→		},
   306→		{
   307→			Type: "assistant",
   308→			UUID: "asst-2",
   309→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   310→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   311→				"usage": map[string]int{
   312→					"input_tokens":                10,
   313→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   314→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   315→					"output_tokens":               5, // More output
   316→				},
   317→			}),
   318→		},
   319→		{
   320→			Type: "assistant",
   321→			UUID: "asst-3",
   322→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   323→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   324→				"usage": map[string]int{
   325→					"input_tokens":                10,
   326→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   327→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   328→					"output_tokens":               20, // Final output
   329→				},
   330→			}),
   331→		},
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   335→
   336→	// Should deduplicate to 1 API call with the highest output_tokens
   337→	if usage.APICallCount != 1 {
   338→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 1 (should deduplicate by message ID)", usage.APICallCount)
   339→	}
   340→	if usage.OutputTokens != 20 {
   341→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 20 (should take highest)", usage.OutputTokens)
   342→	}
   343→	// Input/cache tokens should not be duplicated
   344→	if usage.InputTokens != 10 {
   345→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 10", usage.InputTokens)
   346→	}
   347→}

   537→// mustMarshal is a test helper that marshals a value to JSON or fails the test
   538→func mustMarshal(t *testing.T, v interface{}) []byte {
   539→	t.Helper()
   540→	data, err := json.Marshal(v)
   541→	if err != nil {
   542→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)

   547→// TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint verifies token usage
   548→// is calculated per-checkpoint, not from the full conversation.
   549→// This tests the core CalculateTotalTokenUsage function which should:
   550→// - From line 0: count all turns
   551→// - From line N: count only turns from line N onwards
   552→func TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint(t *testing.T) {
   553→	t.Parallel()
   554→
   555→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   556→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   557→
   558→	// Build transcript with 3 turns:
   559→	// Turn 1: user + assistant (100 input, 50 output)
   560→	// Turn 2: user + assistant (200 input, 100 output)
   561→	// Turn 3: user + assistant (300 input, 150 output)
   562→	//
   563→	// Lines:
   564→	// 0: user message 1
   565→	// 1: assistant response 1 (100/50 tokens)
   566→	// 2: user message 2
   567→	// 3: assistant response 2 (200/100 tokens)
   568→	// 4: user message 3
   569→	// 5: assistant response 3 (300/150 tokens)
   570→
   571→	transcriptContent := []byte(
   572→		`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   573→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"id":"m1","usage":{"input_tokens":100,"output_tokens":50}}}` + "\n" +
   574→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   575→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"id":"m2","usage":{"input_tokens":200,"output_tokens":100}}}` + "\n" +
   576→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u3","message":{"content":"third prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   577→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a3","message":{"id":"m3","usage":{"input_tokens":300,"output_tokens":150}}}` + "\n",
   578→	)
   579→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, transcriptContent, 0o600); err != nil {
   580→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Test 1: From line 0 - all 3 turns = 600 input, 300 output
   584→	usage1, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 0, "")
   585→	if err != nil {
   586→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(0) error: %v", err)
   587→	}
   588→	if usage1.InputTokens != 600 || usage1.OutputTokens != 300 {
   589→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got input=%d output=%d, want input=600 output=300",
   590→			usage1.InputTokens, usage1.OutputTokens)
   591→	}
   592→	if usage1.APICallCount != 3 {
   593→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got APICallCount=%d, want 3", usage1.APICallCount)
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Test 2: From line 2 (after turn 1) - turns 2+3 only = 500 input, 250 output
   597→	usage2, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 2, "")
   598→	if err != nil {
   599→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(2) error: %v", err)
   600→	}
   601→	if usage2.InputTokens != 500 || usage2.OutputTokens != 250 {
   602→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got input=%d output=%d, want input=500 output=250",
   603→			usage2.InputTokens, usage2.OutputTokens)
   604→	}
   605→	if usage2.APICallCount != 2 {
   606→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got APICallCount=%d, want 2", usage2.APICallCount)
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Test 3: From line 4 (after turns 1+2) - turn 3 only = 300 input, 150 output
   610→	usage3, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 4, "")
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(4) error: %v", err)
   613→	}
   614→	if usage3.InputTokens != 300 || usage3.OutputTokens != 150 {
   615→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got input=%d output=%d, want input=300 output=150",
   616→			usage3.InputTokens, usage3.OutputTokens)
   617→	}
   618→	if usage3.APICallCount != 1 {
   619→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got APICallCount=%d, want 1", usage3.APICallCount)
   620→	}
   621→}
   622→
   623→// writeJSONLFile is a test helper that writes JSONL transcript lines to a file.
   624→func writeJSONLFile(t *testing.T, path string, lines ...string) {
   625→	t.Helper()
   626→	var buf strings.Builder
   627→	for _, line := range lines {
   628→		buf.WriteString(line)
   629→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
   630→	}
   631→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(buf.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   632→		t.Fatalf("failed to write JSONL file %s: %v", path, err)
   633→	}
   634→}
   635→
   636→// makeWriteToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Write tool_use for the given file.
   637→func makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
   638→	t.Helper()
   639→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   640→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   641→			{
   642→				"type":  "tool_use",
   643→				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
   644→				"name":  "Write",
   645→				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
   646→			},
   647→		},
   648→	})
   649→	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   650→		"type":    "assistant",
   651→		"uuid":    uuid,
   652→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   653→	})
   654→	return string(line)
   655→}

   719→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_IncludesSubagentFiles(t *testing.T) {
   720→	t.Parallel()
   721→
   722→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   723→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   724→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   725→
   726→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   727→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   728→	}
   729→
   730→	// Main transcript: Write to main.go + Task call spawning subagent "sub1"
   731→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   732→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/main.go"),
   733→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   734→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   735→	)
   736→
   737→	// Subagent transcript: Write to helper.go + Edit to utils.go
   738→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   739→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/helper.go"),
   740→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/utils.go"),
   741→	)
   742→
   743→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   744→	if err != nil {
   745→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   746→	}
   747→
   748→	if len(files) != 3 {
   749→		t.Errorf("expected 3 files, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
   753→		"/repo/main.go":   true,
   754→		"/repo/helper.go": true,
   755→		"/repo/utils.go":  true,
   756→	}
   757→	for _, f := range files {
   758→		if !wantFiles[f] {
   759→			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
   760→		}
   761→		delete(wantFiles, f)
   762→	}
   763→	for f := range wantFiles {
   764→		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
   765→	}
   766→}
   767→
   768→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_DeduplicatesAcrossAgents(t *testing.T) {
   769→	t.Parallel()
   770→
   771→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   772→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   773→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   774→
   775→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   776→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   777→	}
   778→
   779→	// Main transcript: Write to shared.go + Task call
   780→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   781→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   782→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   783→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   784→	)
   785→
   786→	// Subagent transcript: Also modifies shared.go (same file as main)
   787→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   788→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   789→	)
   790→
   791→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	if len(files) != 1 {
   797→		t.Errorf("expected 1 file (deduplicated), got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   798→	}
   799→	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/shared.go" {
   800→		t.Errorf("expected /repo/shared.go, got %q", files[0])
   801→	}
   802→}

   804→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_NoSubagents(t *testing.T) {

   828→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {

Only main files\n17. `REDACTED` - Only subagent files (main has no file writes)\n\n**Helper functions** (same pattern as Claude Code transcript_test.go):\n```go\nfunc mustMarshal(t *testing.T, v interface{}) []byte {\n    t.Helper()\n    data, err := json.Marshal(v)\n    if err != nil { t.Fatalf(\"failed to marshal: %v\", err) }\n    return data\n}\n\nfunc writeJSONLFile(t *testing.T, path string, lines ...string) {\n    t.Helper()\n    var buf strings.Builder\n    for _, line := range lines {\n        buf.WriteString(line)\n        buf.WriteByte('\\n')\n    }\n    if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(buf.String()), 0o600); err != nil {\n        t.Fatalf(\"failed to write JSONL file %s: %v\", path, err)\n    }\n}\n\nfunc makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string { ... }\nfunc makeEditToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string { ... }\nfunc makeTaskToolUseLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID string) string { ... }\nfunc makeTaskResultLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID, agentID string) string { ... }\n```\n\nThe makeXxxLine helpers create JSONL lines with nested message structure. Example:\n```go\nfunc makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {\n    t.Helper()\n    data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{\n        \"content\": []map[string]interface{}{\n            {\"type\": \"tool_use\", \"id\": \"toolu_\" + uuid, \"name\": \"Write\", \"input\": map[string]string{\"file_path\": filePath}},\n        },\n    })\n    line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{\n        \"type\": \"assistant\", \"uuid\": uuid, \"message\": json.RawMessage(data),\n    })\n    return string(line)\n}\n```\n\n**Transcript line format** (JSONL):\n```json\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"a1\",\"message\":{\"id\":\"msg_001\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"name\":\"Write\",\"input\":{\"file_path\":\"foo.go\"}}],\"usage\":{\"input_tokens\":10,\"output_tokens\":20}}}\n```\n\nFor token usage tests, the message structure is:\n```json\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"a1\",\"message\":{\"id\":\"msg_001\",\"usage\":{\"input_tokens\":10,\"cache_creation_input_tokens\":100,\"cache_read_input_tokens\":50,\"output_tokens\":20}}}\n```\n\n**Imports**: `encoding/json`, `os`, `strings`, `testing`, `github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript`\n\nVerify the file compiles with `go build` and tests pass with `go test`.\n"
}

**File created:** `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks_test.go`

**16 test functions:**

| # | Test | What it verifies |
|---|------|-----------------|
| 1 | `TestInstallHooks_FreshInstall` | Installs 7 hooks on empty dir, verifies each hook command, confirms `AreHooksInstalled()` returns true |
| 2 | `TestInstallHooks_Idempotent` | Second install returns count 0, no duplicate hooks |
| 3 | `TestInstallHooks_LocalDev` | `localDev=true` produces `go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ...` commands |
| 4 | `TestInstallHooks_Force` | Force reinstall replaces all 7 hooks |
| 5 | `TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_FreshInstall` | Deny rule `Read(./.entire/metadata/**)` is added |
| 6 | `TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_Idempotent` | Only 1 copy of deny rule after double install |
| 7 | `TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUserRules` | Existing user deny rules preserved alongside ours |
| 8 | `TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUnknownFields` | Unknown permission fields (`ask`, `customField`) preserved |
| 9 | `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType` | User hooks on Stop, SessionStart, PostToolUse preserved alongside Entire hooks (subtests with `t.Parallel()`) |
| 10 | `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` | Unknown hook types (Notification, SubagentStop) preserved |
| 11 | `TestUninstallHooks` | Install then uninstall, `AreHooksInstalled()` returns false |
| 12 | `TestUninstallHooks_NoSettingsFile` | No error when settings file does not exist |
| 13 | `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserHooks` | Only Entire hooks removed, user hooks preserved |
| 14 | `TestUninstallHooks_RemovesDenyRule` | Deny rule removed on uninstall |
| 15 | `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserDenyRules` | User deny rules preserved when Entire deny rule removed |
| 16 | `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` | Unknown hook types preserved during uninstall |

**Helper functions:**
- `writeFactorySettingsFile` - writes a `.factory/settings.json` file with given content
- `readFactoryPermissions` - reads and parses permissions from the settings file
- `readFactorySettings` - reads and parses the full `FactorySettings` struct
- `assertFactoryHookExists` - asserts a hook with given matcher and command exists
- `containsRule` - checks if a rule exists in a slice using `slices.Contains`

**Key patterns followed:**
- No `t.Parallel()` on tests using `t.Chdir()` (with `//nolint:tparallel` comments removed by gofmt where they were on the same line as the func signature, kept where they were on a separate line before `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType`)
- Uses `testutil.ReadRawHooks(t, tempDir, ".factory")` for raw hook inspection
- Uses `testutil.GetKeys()` for map key reporting
- Settings directory is `.factory/` with file `settings.json`

## Batch 1 Complete - Summary

All 9 tasks are done. Here's what was implemented:

**What was built:**
- Complete Factory AI Droid agent integration with full feature parity to Claude Code

**What was verified:**
- `mise run fmt` - clean
- `mise run lint` - 0 issues
- `mise run test:ci` - all tests pass (including integration tests)
- 45 new tests in the factoryaidroid package, all passing

**Files created/modified:**

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/types.go` | Created - hook input structs, settings types, tool constants |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/factoryaidroid.go` | Created - core agent struct, identity, transcript storage |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle.go` | Created - ParseHookEvent, TranscriptAnalyzer, TokenCalculator, etc. |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle_test.go` | Created - 12 tests for hook event parsing |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks.go` | Created - HookSupport (install/uninstall/detect), 9 hook names |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/hooks_test.go` | Created - 16 tests for hook installation |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/transcript.go` | Created - transcript analysis, token calculation, subagent extraction |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/transcript_test.go` | Created - 17 tests for transcript analysis |
| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` | Modified - added `AgentNameFactoryAIDroid` and `AgentTypeFactoryAIDroid` constants |
| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go` | Modified - added blank import for agent registration |
| `cmd/entire/cli/config.go` | Modified - added blank import for agent detection |
| `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/summarize.go` | Modified - added FactoryAIDroid to exhaustive switch |
| `CLAUDE.md` | Modified - updated E2E agent list |

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Architecture pattern**: The agent package uses **inversion of control** — each agent self-registers via `init()` and blank imports. The framework discovers agents at runtime through the registry, calling `ParseHookEvent()` to normalize native hooks into a common `Event` type. This is why adding a new agent requires zero changes to the core orchestration code — only blank imports to trigger `init()`.

**Hook event normalization**: Factory AI Droid's 9 hooks map cleanly to the same lifecycle events as Claude Code. Two hooks (SubagentStop, Notification) return `nil` — acknowledged but no lifecycle action needed. This "pass-through" pattern keeps the agent from needing to understand lifecycle semantics.

**Transcript flush sentinel**: The `waitForTranscriptFlush` function polls for a sentinel string in the JSONL transcript, matching timestamps within a 2-second skew window. This handles the async nature of Factory Droid's transcript writing — without it, hook handlers might read incomplete transcripts.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

what is finishing-a-development-branch skill

> AGENT

It's a skill from the superpowers plugin that guides completing development work after all tasks pass. It:

1. Runs final verification (fmt, lint, tests)
2. Presents structured options for how to integrate the work:
   - **Merge** directly into the base branch
   - **Create a PR** 
   - **Leave on branch** for later
3. Handles the chosen option (commit, push, PR creation, etc.)

Basically it's a checklist to make sure nothing is missed before wrapping up a feature branch. Since all tests already pass, it would mostly just ask how you want to land the changes.

Want me to run it, or would you prefer to just commit and/or create a PR directly?

All changes are complete. Here is a summary of all refinements made across the Factory AI Droid agent files:

---

## Summary of Changes

### `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/factoryaidroid.go`

1. **Added package doc comment** -- `// Package factoryaidroid implements the Agent interface for Factory AI Droid.` to match the Claude Code agent pattern (`// Package claudecode implements the Agent interface for Claude Code.`).

2. **Replaced section separator comments with proper Go doc comments** -- The `// --- Identity ---`, `// --- Transcript Storage ---`, and `// --- Legacy methods ---` separators were replaced with individual doc comments on each exported method (`Name`, `Type`, `Description`, `ProtectedDirs`, `DetectPresence`, `ReadTranscript`, `ChunkTranscript`, `ReassembleTranscript`, `GetHookConfigPath`, `SupportsHooks`, `ParseHookInput`, `GetSessionID`, `GetSessionDir`, `ResolveSessionFile`, `ReadSession`, `WriteSession`, `FormatResumeCommand`). Every exported method now has a doc comment consistent with the Claude Code agent pattern.

### `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/types.go`

3. **Removed duplicate `preCompactRaw` type** -- The `preCompactRaw` struct had identical fields to `sessionInfoRaw`. Replaced its single usage in `lifecycle.go`'s `parseCompaction` with `sessionInfoRaw` and updated the `sessionInfoRaw` doc comment to include PreCompact in the list of hooks that use it.

### `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/lifecycle.go`

4. **Updated `parseCompaction` to use `sessionInfoRaw`** -- Changed `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[preCompactRaw]` to `agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw]`, eliminating the need for the now-removed duplicate type.

### `/Users/alisha/Projects/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/transcript.go`

5. **Simplified `ExtractModifiedFiles` tool check** -- Replaced the manual loop with `slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, block.Name)`, reducing 8 lines to a single condition. Added `slices` import.

6. **Fixed `textContent += sb.String()` to `textContent = sb.String()`** in `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs` -- Since `textContent` starts as an empty string and this is the only assignment in the branch, `+=` was misleading. Changed to `=` for clarity.
